## 17 CFR 244.100(a)：Regulation G 非 GAAP 财务指标的列报与调节（核实于 2026-11-12）

上面已经打包的 10D-1 保持原样。不要改写那个已上线的框。这一节只打包官方 244.100(a)，避免把官方 (b) 的 “in light of the circumstances under which it is presented, not misleading” 改整齐成已经打包的 10b-5 / 12b-20 的 “in the light of” 或 “they are presented，” 把 “by Web cast、” “the registrant's Web site、” “the location of the web site” 或 “web sites” 改整齐成 “website、” “webcast、” 已经打包的 4370 / 2264 的 “Web site，” 或已经打包的 3150 的 “website，” 悄悄改写官方 Note 1 对 “paragraphs (a)(1)(i) and (a)(1)(ii)” 的引用，或把本规则压进已经教过的业绩 / 10-K / 10-Q 时钟、已经打包的 13a-14、已经打包的 12b-20 或已经打包的 10D-1。Title 17 截至 2026-11-09。仅供学习；没有发行人、披露或投资建议。

[17 CFR 244.100](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-244.100) 官方标题印刷是 “General rules regarding disclosure of non-GAAP financial measures。” 官方 (a)：“Whenever a registrant, or person acting on its behalf, publicly discloses material information that includes a non-GAAP financial measure, the registrant must accompany that non-GAAP financial measure with: (1) A presentation of the most directly comparable financial measure calculated and presented in accordance with Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (GAAP); and (2) A reconciliation (by schedule or other clearly understandable method), which shall be quantitative for historical non-GAAP measures presented, and quantitative, to the extent available without unreasonable efforts, for forward-looking information, of the differences between the non-GAAP financial measure disclosed or released with the most comparable financial measure or measures calculated and presented in accordance with GAAP identified in paragraph (a)(1) of this section.” 官方 (a)(1) 印刷是首字母大写的 “Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (GAAP)。” 官方 (c)(2) 后来使用小写的 “generally accepted accounting principles in the United States”；不要把其中一种改整齐成另一种。

点名官方 (b)。官方 (b) 印刷是 “in light of the circumstances under which it is presented, not misleading”（不是已经打包的 10b-5 / 12b-20 的 “in the light of，” 不是 12b-20 的 “they are made，” 也不是 “they are presented”）。点名官方 (c) 和官方 (d)。官方 (d) 印刷是 “the entity resulting therefrom or an entity that is a party thereto，” 没有牛津逗号。官方 Note 1 印刷是 “by Web cast”（两个单词，大写 W），并引用 “paragraphs (a)(1)(i) and (a)(1)(ii)，” 尽管官方 (a) 的编号是 (a)(1)/(a)(2)；不要悄悄改写该注的编号。官方 Note 1(i) 印刷是 “the registrant's Web site”（大写 W，两个单词）；官方 Note 1(ii) 印刷是 “the location of the web site”（小写 w，两个单词）；官方 Note 2(iii) 印刷是 “web sites”（小写）。不要把这些改整齐成 “website” / “webcast、” 已经打包的 4370 / 2264 的 “Web site，” 或已经打包的 3150 的 “website。” 17 CFR 244.101 / 244.102 以及 Regulation S-K Item 10(e) / 17 CFR 229.10 只点名；不要把它们当成第二个核心打包，也不要把 244.102 压进已经打包的 10b-5。若点名官方 244.102，印刷包括 “compliance or non-compliance” 和 “shall in itself affect。” 不要把 244.100 压进已经教过的业绩 / 10-K / 10-Q 时钟、已经打包的 13a-14、已经打包的 12b-20 或已经打包的 10D-1。

**实验：** 同一脚本的 J 节（前面的 **34/34** + 10D-1 结尾免责声明保持不变；J 节之后为 37/37，并在最后一行之后带有结尾免责声明）：

```text
=== J. 17 CFR 244.100(a): Regulation G non-GAAP financial measure presentation and reconciliation ===
244.100(a): Whenever a registrant, or person acting on its behalf, publicly discloses
  material information that includes a non-GAAP financial measure, the registrant must
  accompany that non-GAAP financial measure with:
  (1) A presentation of the most directly comparable financial measure calculated and
  presented in accordance with Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (GAAP); and
  (2) A reconciliation (by schedule or other clearly understandable method), which shall
  be quantitative for historical non-GAAP measures presented, and quantitative, to the
  extent available without unreasonable efforts, for forward-looking information, of
  the differences between the non-GAAP financial measure disclosed or released with the
  most comparable financial measure or measures calculated and presented in accordance
  with GAAP identified in paragraph (a)(1) of this section.
official (a)(1) typography is "Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (GAAP)" with
  initial capitals; official (c)(2) later uses lowercase "generally accepted accounting
  principles in the United States"; this toy does not tidy one into the other
official caption is General rules regarding disclosure of non-GAAP financial measures.
cite 17 CFR 244.100; title 17 up_to_date_as_of 2026-11-09
official (b) typography is "in light of the circumstances under which it is presented,
  not misleading" (not packed 10b-5 / 12b-20 "in the light of," and not 12b-20's "they
  are made," and not "they are presented"); official (b) is named only
official (c) FPI conditions and official (d) proposed-business-combination exception
  exist; official (d) typography is "the entity resulting therefrom or an entity that
  is a party thereto" (no Oxford comma); this toy is not those catalogs
official notes heading is Notes to § 244.100:
official Note 1 typography is "orally, telephonically, by Web cast, by broadcast, or by
  similar means" (two-word "Web cast," capital W, Oxford comma) and cites "paragraphs
  (a)(1)(i) and (a)(1)(ii) of this section" even though official (a) is numbered (a)(1)
  and (a)(2); this toy does not silently renumber the official note
official Note 1(i) typography is "the registrant's Web site" (capital W, two words);
  official Note 1(ii) typography is "the location of the web site" (lowercase w, two
  words); official Note 2(iii) typography is "web sites" (lowercase); this toy does not
  tidy those into "website" or "webcast," packed 4370 / 2264 "Web site," or packed 3150
  "website"
17 CFR 244.101 (definitions, including official (a) Non-GAAP financial measure), 17 CFR
  244.102 ("No effect on antifraud liability"; official typography "compliance or
  non-compliance," "shall in itself affect"), and Regulation S-K Item 10(e) / 17 CFR
  229.10 exist and are named only; this toy does not pack them as second cores, and
  does not flatten 244.102 into packed 10b-5
  this is a flatten into already-taught earnings / 10-K / 10-Q clocks, packed 13a-14, packed 12b-20, or packed 10D-1? False
  this toy tidies official (b) into "in the light of" or "they are presented", tidies "Web cast" / "Web site" / "web site" into "website" or "webcast", or silently renumbers official Note 1's (a)(1)(i) and (a)(1)(ii)? False
  whenever a registrant publicly discloses material information that includes a non-GAAP financial measure, the registrant must accompany that measure with the most directly comparable GAAP measure and a reconciliation? True
learning only; a non-GAAP figure is not a substitute for the accompanying GAAP measure and reconciliation
no investment advice and no use / avoid non-GAAP product recommendation
assertions passed: 37/37
disclaimer: official 17 CFR 244.100; no issuer, disclosure, or investment advice; learning only
```

## 17 CFR 244.102：对反欺诈责任没有影响（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 244.100 保持原样；官方 244.101 只被点名。已经打包的 243.102、已经打包的 10b-5，以及已经教过的 Regulation FD 时钟，也保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：“Neither the requirements of this Regulation G (17 CFR 244.100 through 244.102) nor a person's compliance or non-compliance with the requirements of this Regulation shall in itself affect any person's liability under Section 10(b) (15 U.S.C. 78j(b)) of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934 or § 240.10b-5 of this chapter.” 官方印刷是 “17 CFR 244.100 through 244.102”（用 “through，” 不是 “to”）、“compliance or non-compliance，” 以及 “shall in itself affect.” 官方 “Section 10(b)” 的 S 是大写。官方标题印刷是 “No effect on antifraud liability.”——用词与已经打包的 243.102 相同，但本框是 Regulation G 而不是 Regulation FD；不要把这一操作句改整齐成已经打包的 243.102 的 “No failure to make a public disclosure required solely by § 243.100 shall be deemed to be a violation of Rule 10b-5 (17 CFR 240.10b-5) under the Securities Exchange Act.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA 元素；不要发明来源行。不要改写已经打包的 244.100 框，不要把被点名的 244.101 一并打包，也不要把本规则压进已经打包的 10b-5 或已经打包的 243.102。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 244.102](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-244.102)：“Neither the requirements of this Regulation G (17 CFR 244.100 through 244.102) nor a person's compliance or non-compliance with the requirements of this Regulation shall in itself affect any person's liability under Section 10(b) (15 U.S.C. 78j(b)) of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934 or § 240.10b-5 of this chapter.” 官方标题印刷是 “No effect on antifraud liability.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA 元素；本课不发明来源行。 Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 244.100 仅点名，本框不改写该框。官方 244.101 仅点名，本框不打包该定义目录。已经打包的 243.102 和已经打包的 10b-5 仅点名。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 AF 节（接在第 AE 节 / 15d-6 之后；AF 之后为 117/117）：

```text
=== AF. 17 CFR 244.102: No effect on antifraud liability ===
official caption typography is No effect on antifraud liability.
244.102: Neither the requirements of this Regulation G (17 CFR 244.100 through 244.102) nor a person's compliance or non-compliance with the requirements of this Regulation shall in itself affect any person's liability under Section 10(b) (15 U.S.C. 78j(b)) of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934 or § 240.10b-5 of this chapter.
official typography is "17 CFR 244.100 through 244.102"
official typography is "compliance or non-compliance"
official typography is "shall in itself affect"
official typography is capital-S "Section 10(b)"
official API XML has no CITA element; this toy does not invent a source line
cite 17 CFR 244.102; title 17 up_to_date_as_of 2026-11-17
  neither the requirements of this Regulation G (17 CFR 244.100 through 244.102) nor a person's compliance or non-compliance with the requirements of this Regulation shall in itself affect any person's liability under Section 10(b) (15 U.S.C. 78j(b)) of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934 or § 240.10b-5 of this chapter? True

official 244.101 is named only; this toy does not pack that definitions catalog
packed 243.102 and packed 10b-5 exist and are named only; this toy is Regulation G, not those boxes

no issuer, filer, or investment advice
```

## 17 CFR 240.14a-3(b)：随附或先于委托书送达证券持有人的年度报告（核实于 2026-11-12）

上面已经打包的 Regulation G / 244.100(a) 保持原样。本课前面已经打包的 14a-9 / 12b-20 也保持原样。这一节只打包官方 14a-3(b) 的开句，到 “as follows:” 为止。这是一条数据时点事实：在官方 (b) 所说的那次会议上，给证券持有人的年度报告随附或先于委托书说明书。它不是 Form 10-K 时钟，也不是已经打包的 14a-9。不要发明每一次委托书寄送都需要年度报告。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 14a-9、已经打包的 12b-20、已经教过的 14a-8、Form 8-K Item 5.07、已经教过的 10-K / 10-Q 时钟、已经打包的 13a-14 或已经打包的 244.100，也不要把给证券持有人的年度报告当成与 Form 10-K 同一份申报。仅供学习；没有发行人、委托书或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.14a-3](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.14a-3)(b) 开句：“If the solicitation is made on behalf of the registrant, other than an investment company registered under the Investment Company Act of 1940, and relates to an annual (or special meeting in lieu of the annual) meeting of security holders, or written consent in lieu of such meeting, at which directors are to be elected, each proxy statement furnished pursuant to paragraph (a) of this section shall be accompanied or preceded by an annual report to security holders as follows:” 官方标题印刷是 “Information to be furnished to security holders。” 官方印刷是两词的 “security holders，” 以及 “accompanied or preceded。” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-09。

官方 (a)（委托书说明书可以提供的三种方式）、官方 (b)(1) 及以后（包括财务报表目录）、官方 14a-3(e) 家庭合并寄送（已经从 14b-1 交叉点名），以及 17 CFR 240.14a-4 / 14a-6（委托书表格 / 申报目录）是存在的；点名它们，不要搭建那些目录。若引用官方 (b)(1)，官方印刷包括笔误 “this paragraph 9(b)(1)”；不要改掉那个笔误。官方 (b) 仅限于以注册人的名义、且不是已注册投资公司、并涉及将选举董事的年度会议（或以特别会议代替年度会议），或代替该会议的书面同意的征集。

**实验：** `examples/p2/16_index_add_earnings_time_toy.py` 的 K 节（前面的 **37/37 after J / 244.100(a)** + 收尾免责声明保持不变；K 节之后为 40/40）：

```text
=== K. 17 CFR 240.14a-3(b): annual report to security holders accompanying or preceding the proxy ===
official caption typography is Information to be furnished to security holders.
14a-3(b) opening: If the solicitation is made on behalf of the registrant, other than
  an investment company registered under the Investment Company Act of 1940, and
  relates to an annual (or special meeting in lieu of the annual) meeting of
  security holders, or written consent in lieu of such meeting, at which directors
  are to be elected, each proxy statement furnished pursuant to paragraph (a) of
  this section shall be accompanied or preceded by an annual report to security
  holders as follows:
official typography is two-word "security holders" and "accompanied or preceded"
official (a) (the three ways a proxy statement may be furnished), official (b)(1)
  and later, including the financial-statement catalog, official 14a-3(e)
  householding, and 17 CFR 240.14a-4 / 14a-6 exist and are named only; this toy is
  not those catalogs
if official (b)(1) is quoted, official typography includes the typo "this paragraph
  9(b)(1)"; this toy does not tidy that typo
cite 17 CFR 240.14a-3; title 17 up_to_date_as_of 2026-11-09
  this is a flatten into packed 14a-9, packed 12b-20, already-taught 14a-8, Form 8-K Item 5.07, already-taught 10-K / 10-Q clocks, packed 13a-14, or packed 244.100, or a claim that the annual report to security holders is the same filing as the Form 10-K? False
  this toy invents that every proxy mailing requires an annual report? False

True

no issuer, proxy, or investment advice
```

## 17 CFR 240.14c-3(a)：随附或先于信息说明书送达证券持有人的年度报告（核实于 2026-11-12）

已经打包的 14a-3(b) / 14a-9 / 12b-20 保持原样。这一节只打包官方 14c-3(a) 开句，到 “security holders:” 为止。这是已经打包的 14a-3(b) 在信息说明书一侧的配套：就该官方 (a) 会议而言，给证券持有人的年度报告随附或先于信息说明书送达。官方 (a) 的词序与已经打包的 14a-3(b) 并不相同；不要把 14a-3(b) 那一句贴到这里。不要发明每一份信息说明书都需要年度报告。不要发明 14c-3(a) 与已经打包的 14a-3(b) 是同一条规则，或给证券持有人的年度报告就是 Form 10-K。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 14a-3(b)、已经打包的 14a-9、已经打包的 12b-20、已经教过的 14a-8、Form 8-K Item 5.07、已经教过的 10-K / 10-Q 时钟、已经打包的 13a-14，或已经打包的 244.100。把 14c-3(c) 的合并送达当成新的核心来打包仍然是错的。仅供学习；没有发行人、信息说明书或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.14c-3](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.14c-3)(a) 开句：“If the information statement relates to an annual (or special meeting in lieu of the annual) meeting, or written consent in lieu of such meeting, of security holders at which directors of the registrant, other than an investment company registered under the Investment Company Act of 1940, are to be elected, it shall be accompanied or preceded by an annual report to security holders:” 官方标题印刷是 “Annual report to be furnished security holders.”——在 “security holders” 前面**没有 “to，”** 并且在 “holders” 后面有句点。官方印刷是两词的 “security holders” 以及 “accompanied or preceded。” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-09。

官方 (a)(1)（交叉引用 14a-3(b)(1) 至 (b)(11) 的财务报表目录）、官方 (a)(2) “[Reserved]、” 官方 (b)（电子格式提交 / “filed” 两侧的弯引号）、官方 (c) 合并送达（已经在打包的 14b-1 下点名为 14a-3(e)(1) / 14c-3(c)）、官方 (d)（点名 14a-16）、17 CFR 240.14c-2 以及 17 CFR 240.14c-5 是存在的，只点名。官方 (a) 仅限于将选举注册人董事的年度会议（或以特别会议代替年度会议），或代替该等会议的书面同意，且该注册人不是已注册的投资公司。

**实验：** `examples/p2/16_index_add_earnings_time_toy.py` 的 L 节（包括此前的 **K / 14a-3(b) 之后 40/40** + 收尾免责声明；L 节之后为 43/43）：

## 17 CFR 240.14c-6：虚假或误导性陈述（核实于 2026-11-12）

已经打包的 14a-9 / 14c-3(a) / 12b-20 保持原样。这一节打包官方 14c-6(a) 和官方 (b)（整条现行规则）。这是已经打包的 14a-9 的信息说明书配套规则。它不是已经打包的 14a-9，不是已经打包的 14c-3(a)，也不是已经打包的 12b-20。官方 14c-6 没有官方 (c)，也没有 Note；已经打包的 14a-9 的官方 (c) / Note 目录仍然只在那一框下点名。不要把已经打包的 14a-9(a) 或 14a-9(b) 贴到本规则上。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 14a-9、已经打包的 14c-3(a)、已经打包的 12b-20、已经打包的 10b-5、已经教过的 14a-8，或 Form 8-K Item 5.07。仅供学习；没有发行人、信息说明书或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.14c-6](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.14c-6) 打包官方 (a) 和官方 (b)。官方标题印刷是 “False or misleading statements。”

官方 (a)：“No information statement shall contain any statement which, at the time and in the light of the circumstances under which it is made, is false or misleading with respect to any material fact, or which omits to state any material fact necessary in order to make the statements therein not false or misleading or necessary to correct any statement in any earlier communication with respect to the same meeting or subject matter which has become false or misleading.” 官方印刷是 “in the light of the circumstances under which it is made”（与已经打包的 14a-9(a) 一样是单数的 “it is made”；不要把它改整齐成已经打包的 10b-5 的 “under which they were made”，或已经打包的 12b-20 的 “under which they are made not misleading”）。官方印刷是 “any earlier communication with respect to the same meeting or subject matter”——**不是**已经打包的 14a-9(a) 的 “with respect to the solicitation of a proxy for the same meeting or subject matter。” 官方 (a) 限于 “No information statement shall contain”，并不使用已经打包的 14a-9 的 “proxy statement, form of proxy, notice of meeting or other communication, written or oral。”

官方 (b)：“The fact that an information statement has been filed with or examined by the Commission shall not be deemed a finding by the Commission that such material is accurate or complete or not false or misleading, or that the Commission has passed upon the merits of or approved any statement contained therein or any matter to be acted upon by security holders. No representation contrary to the foregoing shall be made.” 官方印刷是 “an information statement has been filed with or examined by the Commission”——**不是**已经打包的 14a-9(b) 的 “a proxy statement, form of proxy or other soliciting material。” 官方印刷是两词的 “security holders.” 官方出处行是 “[31 FR 262, Jan. 8, 1966].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-09。

已经打包的 14a-9（包括官方 (b)、官方 (c) 和 Note，这些仍然只在那一已印框下点名——不要把 14a-9(b) 当成新的核心来打包）、已经打包的 14c-3(a)、已经打包的 12b-20、已经打包的 10b-5、仍作遗留的 14c-2 / 14c-5，以及 17 CFR 240.14c-4 是存在的，只点名。

**实验：** `examples/p2/16_index_add_earnings_time_toy.py` 的 M 节（包括此前的 **L / 14c-3(a) 之后 43/43** + 收尾免责声明；M 节之后为 46/46）：

## 17 CFR 240.15c1-9：备考资产负债表的使用（核实于 2026-11-12）

本课前面打包的 244.100 / Regulation G 保持原样。这个框是另一项备考标题义务，不是 244.100 框的第二个核心。不要为 “at least as large as” 发明数字字号。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 244.100、已经打包的 12b-20、已经打包的 14c-6、已经打包的 15c1-3，或已经教过的 10-K / 10-Q 时钟。官方印刷把这个被定义的用语标成斜体，包括后面的逗号，并且**不**给它加上引号。仅供学习；没有发行人、券商或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.15c1-9](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.15c1-9)：“The term <em>manipulative, deceptive, or other fraudulent device or contrivance,</em> as used in section 15(c)(1) of the Act, is hereby defined to include the use of financial statements purporting to give effect to the receipt and application of any part of the proceeds from the sale or exchange of securities, unless the assumptions upon which each such financial statement is based are clearly set forth as part of the caption to each such statement in type at least as large as that used generally in the body of the statement.” 官方标题印刷是 “Use of pro forma balance sheets。” 官方印刷是两词的 “pro forma”（没有连字符）。官方印刷把这个被定义的用语标成斜体，包括后面的逗号，并且**不**给它加上引号。官方印刷是 “as part of the caption to each such statement in type at least as large as that used generally in the body of the statement”（没有数字字号）。官方授权印刷是 `15 U.S.C. 78<em>o</em>`（官方斜体字母 O / “78o” 形式）。不要在该引用里发明空格，也不要把 LII 的 U.S.C. 链接换行当成会多出一个空格的官方印刷。官方出处行是 “[13 FR 8205, Dec. 22, 1948].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-09。

已经打包的 244.100 / 244.101 / 244.102 以及 Regulation S-K Item 10(e) / 17 CFR 229.10、已经打包的 15c1-3（本批前面）、仍作遗留的 15c1-8，以及 17 CFR 240.15c1-1 是存在的，只点名。

**实验：** 同一脚本的 N 节（在 M / 14c-6 之后，并且仍在 tip 的 L / 14c-3(a) 前缀之后；N 节之后为 49/49）：

```text
  statement.

  15c2-4 typographic quotes onto this rule

[13 FR 8205, Dec. 22, 1948].
```

## 17 CFR 240.12b-15：修订（核实于 2026-11-12）

已经印出的 XBRL 10-K / 10-K/A 例子保持原样；这个框是官方的修订印刷，不是 XBRL 框的第二个核心。不要发明 “10-K/A” 是唯一的修订表格（官方印刷是 e.g., “10-K/A,”，逗号在右引号里面）。官方 13a-14(a) 修订是 “must include new certifications，” 官方 13a-14(b) 修订是 “must be accompanied by new certifications，” 官方 13a-14(d) 修订是 “must include a new certification”——不要把这三种官方表述改整齐成同一种。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 13a-14、已经教过的 Form 10-K / 10-Q 时钟、已经印出的 XBRL `filed` 日期框、已经打包的 12b-20、已经打包的 14c-6，或已经打包的 244.100。不要改写已经印出的 XBRL 框，也不要搭建 Regulation S-T 引擎。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报代理人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12b-15](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-15)：“All amendments must be filed under cover of the form amended, marked with the letter “A” to designate the document as an amendment, e.g., “10-K/A,” and in compliance with pertinent requirements applicable to statements and reports. Amendments filed pursuant to this section must set forth the complete text of each item as amended. Amendments must be numbered sequentially and be filed separately for each statement or report amended. Amendments to a statement may be filed either before or after registration becomes effective. Amendments must be signed on behalf of the registrant by a duly authorized representative of the registrant. An amendment to any report required to include the certifications as specified in § 240.13a-14(a) or § 240.15d-14(a) must include new certifications by each principal executive and principal financial officer of the registrant, and an amendment to any report required to be accompanied by the certifications as specified in § 240.13a-14(b) or § 240.15d-14(b) must be accompanied by new certifications by each principal executive and principal financial officer of the registrant. An amendment to any report required to include the certifications as specified in § 240.13a-14(d) or § 240.15d-14(d) must include a new certification by an individual specified in § 240.13a-14(e) or § 240.15d-14(e), as applicable. The requirements of the form being amended will govern the number of copies to be filed in connection with a paper format amendment. Electronic filers satisfy the provisions dictating the number of copies by filing one copy of the amendment in electronic format. See § 232.309 of this chapter (Rule 309 of Regulation S-T).” 官方标题印刷是 “Amendments。” 官方印刷在 “A” 两侧以及 “10-K/A,” 两侧使用弯引号（U+201C / U+201D），逗号在**右引号里面**。官方印刷是 “e.g.,”（e.g. 后面有逗号）。官方印刷是 “complete text of each item as amended” 以及 “numbered sequentially and be filed separately for each statement or report amended。” 官方出处行是 “[68 FR 36665, June 18, 2003, as amended at 70 FR 1620, Jan. 7, 2005].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-09。

已经印出的 XBRL `filed` 日期框（包括 Apple 的 10-K / 10-K/A 例子）、已经打包的 13a-14 / 已点名的 15d-14、已经教过的 Form 10-K / 10-Q 时钟，以及 17 CFR 232.309 / Regulation S-T Rule 309（电子一份副本那一句已经出现在打包片段里；不要搭建 Regulation S-T 引擎）是存在的，只点名。

**实验：** 同一脚本的 O 节（在 N / 15c1-9 之后，并且仍在 tip 的 L / 14c-3(a) 前缀之后；O 节之后为 52/52）：

## 17 CFR 240.12b-6：证券何时视为已注册（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 12b-15、已经教过的 Form 10-K / 10-Q 时钟、尚未打包的 12g-1 / 12g-4、已经打包的 Section 16(b)，以及已经教过的 Form 4 时钟，保持原样。这一节是另一条申报日陷阱：一类证券要等到第 12 条注册说明书已经**生效**，才视为就第 13、14、15(d) 和 16 条已注册，也才受第 13、14 和 16 条约束。不要从提交日就开始这些时钟。不要在官方第二份清单里发明 15(d)。不要把本规则压进那些已经上线的框。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报代理或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12b-6](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-6)：“A class of securities with respect to which a registration statement has been filed pursuant to section 12 of the act shall be deemed to be registered for the purposes of sections 13, 14, 15(d) and 16 of the act and the rules and regulations thereunder only when such statement has become effective as provided in section 12, and securities of said class shall not be subject to sections 13, 14 and 16 of the act until such statement has become effective as provided in section 12.” 官方标题印刷是 “When securities are deemed to be registered.” 官方印刷是 “section 12 of the act”（act 小写）。官方印刷是 “sections 13, 14, 15(d) and 16 of the act”（“and 16” 前没有牛津逗号）。官方在第二分句的印刷是 “sections 13, 14 and 16 of the act”（没有 15(d)；没有牛津逗号）。官方授权印刷是 15 U.S.C. 78c, 78n, 78p, 78*l*（斜体字母 L）。官方来源行：“[30 FR 482, Jan. 14, 1965].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 12b-15、已经教过的 Form 10-K / 10-Q 时钟、尚未打包的 12g-1 / 12g-4、已经打包的 Section 16(b)，以及已经教过的 Form 4 时钟，仅点名，不另开核心。

**实验：** `examples/p2/16_index_add_earnings_time_toy.py` 的第 P 节（接在第 O 节 / 12b-15 之后，并仍接在 tip 的 N / 15c1-9 前缀之后；P 之后为 56/56）：

## 17 CFR 240.12d2-1：暂停交易（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 12b-6 和已经教过的退市收益框保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：官方 (a) “A national securities exchange may suspend from trading a security listed and registered thereon in accordance with its rules. Such exchange shall promptly notify the Commission of any such suspension, the effective date thereof, and the reasons therefor.” 不要给 “promptly” 发明一个数字时钟。官方 (b) “Any such suspension may be continued until such time as it shall appear to the Commission that such suspension is designed to evade the provisions of section 12(d) and the rules and regulations thereunder relating to the withdrawal and striking of a security from listing and registration. During the continuance of such suspension the exchange shall notify the Commission promptly of any change in the reasons for the suspension. Upon the restoration to trading of any security suspended under this rule, the exchange shall notify the Commission promptly of the effective date thereof.” 官方 (c) “Suspension of trading shall not terminate the registration of any security.” 官方印刷是 “the reasons therefor，” “designed to evade the provisions of section 12(d)，” 以及 “withdrawal and striking。” 官方 SECAUTH 印刷是 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>，字母 l 保持官方斜体。官方标题印刷是 “Suspension of trading.” 官方来源行：“[28 FR 1506, Feb. 16, 1963].” 官方 12d2-2 仅点名；不要打包那份摘牌目录。不要把本框压进已经打包的 12b-6 或已经教过的退市收益框。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12d2-1](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12d2-1)：官方 (a) “A national securities exchange may suspend from trading a security listed and registered thereon in accordance with its rules. Such exchange shall promptly notify the Commission of any such suspension, the effective date thereof, and the reasons therefor.” 官方 (b) “Any such suspension may be continued until such time as it shall appear to the Commission that such suspension is designed to evade the provisions of section 12(d) and the rules and regulations thereunder relating to the withdrawal and striking of a security from listing and registration. During the continuance of such suspension the exchange shall notify the Commission promptly of any change in the reasons for the suspension. Upon the restoration to trading of any security suspended under this rule, the exchange shall notify the Commission promptly of the effective date thereof.” 官方 (c) “Suspension of trading shall not terminate the registration of any security.” 官方标题印刷是 “Suspension of trading.” 官方印刷是 “the reasons therefor” 以及 “Suspension of trading shall not terminate the registration of any security。” 官方 SECAUTH 印刷是 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>。官方来源行：“[28 FR 1506, Feb. 16, 1963].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

## 17 CFR 240.12d1-1：注册对类别或系列生效（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 12d2-1 和已经打包的 12b-6 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：官方 (a) “An application filed pursuant to section 12 (b) and (c) of the act for registration of a security on a national securities exchange shall be deemed to apply for registration of the entire class of such security. Registration shall become effective, as provided in section 12(d) of the act, (1) as to the shares or amounts of such class then issued, and (2), without further application for registration, upon issuance as to additional shares or amounts of such class then or thereafter authorized.” 官方印刷是 “section 12 (b) and (c) of the act”（字母段落两侧有空格；官方 (a) 用小写 “act”）。官方 (b) “This section shall apply to classes of securities of which a specified number of shares or amounts was registered or registered upon notice of issuance, and to applications for registration filed, prior to the close of business on January 28, 1954, as well as to classes registered, or applications filed, thereafter.” 官方 (c) “This section shall not affect the right of a national securities exchange to require the issuer of a registered security to file documents with or pay fees to the exchange in connection with the modification of such security or the issuance of additional shares or amounts.” 官方 (d) “If a class of security is issuable in two or more series with different terms, each such series shall be deemed a separate class for the purposes of this section.” 官方标题印刷是 “Registration effective as to class or series.” 官方 SECAUTH 印刷是 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>，字母 l 保持官方斜体。官方 API XML 没有 CITA；不要发明来源行。不要把本框压进已经打包的 12d2-1 或已经打包的 12b-6。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12d1-1](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12d1-1)：官方 (a) “An application filed pursuant to section 12 (b) and (c) of the act for registration of a security on a national securities exchange shall be deemed to apply for registration of the entire class of such security. Registration shall become effective, as provided in section 12(d) of the act, (1) as to the shares or amounts of such class then issued, and (2), without further application for registration, upon issuance as to additional shares or amounts of such class then or thereafter authorized.” 官方 (b) “This section shall apply to classes of securities of which a specified number of shares or amounts was registered or registered upon notice of issuance, and to applications for registration filed, prior to the close of business on January 28, 1954, as well as to classes registered, or applications filed, thereafter.” 官方 (c) “This section shall not affect the right of a national securities exchange to require the issuer of a registered security to file documents with or pay fees to the exchange in connection with the modification of such security or the issuance of additional shares or amounts.” 官方 (d) “If a class of security is issuable in two or more series with different terms, each such series shall be deemed a separate class for the purposes of this section.” 官方标题印刷是 “Registration effective as to class or series.” 官方印刷是 “section 12 (b) and (c) of the act” 以及 “a class of security”。官方 SECAUTH 印刷是 “Sec. 12, 48 Stat. 892, as amended; 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA。Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 12d2-1 和已经打包的 12b-6 仅点名。

## 17 CFR 240.12d1-2：注册生效（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 12d2-1 和已经打包的 12b-6 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：Form 8-A 在 12(b) 与 12(g) 下的生效时钟并不相同。官方 (a) “A request for acceleration of the effective date of registration pursuant to section 12(d) of the act and § 240.12d1-1 shall be made in writing by either the registrant, the exchange, or both and shall briefly describe the reasons therefor.” 官方印刷是 “the reasons therefor.” 官方 (b) “A registration statement on Form 8-A (17 CFR 249.208a) for the registration of a class of securities under Section 12(b) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>(b)) shall become effective:” 官方 (b)(1) “If a class of securities is not concurrently being registered under the Securities Act of 1933 (“Securities Act”), upon the later of receipt by the Commission of certification from the national securities exchange or the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission; or” 官方 (b)(2) “If a class of securities is concurrently being registered under the Securities Act, upon the later of the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission, receipt by the Commission of certification from the national securities exchange listed on the Form 8-A or effectiveness of the Securities Act registration statement relating to the class of securities.” 官方 (c) “A registration statement on Form 8-A (17 CFR 249.208a) for the registration of a class of securities under Section 12(g) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>(g)) shall become effective:” 官方 (c)(1) “If a class of securities is not concurrently being registered under the Securities Act, upon the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission; or” 官方 (c)(2) “If class of securities is concurrently being registered under the Securities Act, upon the later of the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission or the effectiveness of the Securities Act registration statement relating to the class of securities.” 官方 (c)(2) 印刷是 “If class of securities is concurrently being registered under the Securities Act”——“If” 后面没有 “a”；官方 (b)(1)、(b)(2) 和 (c)(1) 则用 “If a class of securities”。不要把 (c)(2) 改整齐成 “If a class.” 官方 12(b) 时钟取 Form 8-A 申报与交易所证明中较晚者（若同时进行 Securities Act 注册，还要加上该注册声明生效）；官方 12(g) 时钟不用交易所证明。官方印刷在 “Securities Act” 两侧使用弯引号。官方 (b)/(c) 印刷是 “Section 12(b) of the Act” / “Section 12(g) of the Act”（大写 S 和 Act），以及 Form 8-A（“17 CFR 249.208a”）。官方 SECAUTH 印刷是 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>，包括 78<em>l</em>(b) 和 78<em>l</em>(g)。官方来源行：“[19 FR 670, Feb. 5, 1954, as amended at 59 FR 55347, Nov. 7, 1994; 62 FR 39766, July 24, 1997].” 官方 12d1-3 至 12d1-6 以及官方 12d2-2 仅点名；不要打包那些证明或摘牌目录。不要把本框压进已经打包的 12d2-1 或已经打包的 12b-6。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12d1-2](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12d1-2)：官方 (a) “A request for acceleration of the effective date of registration pursuant to section 12(d) of the act and § 240.12d1-1 shall be made in writing by either the registrant, the exchange, or both and shall briefly describe the reasons therefor.” 官方 (b) “A registration statement on Form 8-A (17 CFR 249.208a) for the registration of a class of securities under Section 12(b) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>(b)) shall become effective:” 官方 (b)(1) “If a class of securities is not concurrently being registered under the Securities Act of 1933 (“Securities Act”), upon the later of receipt by the Commission of certification from the national securities exchange or the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission; or” 官方 (b)(2) “If a class of securities is concurrently being registered under the Securities Act, upon the later of the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission, receipt by the Commission of certification from the national securities exchange listed on the Form 8-A or effectiveness of the Securities Act registration statement relating to the class of securities.” 官方 (c) “A registration statement on Form 8-A (17 CFR 249.208a) for the registration of a class of securities under Section 12(g) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>(g)) shall become effective:” 官方 (c)(1) “If a class of securities is not concurrently being registered under the Securities Act, upon the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission; or” 官方 (c)(2) “If class of securities is concurrently being registered under the Securities Act, upon the later of the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission or the effectiveness of the Securities Act registration statement relating to the class of securities.” 官方标题印刷是 “Effectiveness of registration.” 官方印刷是 “the reasons therefor”，并在 “Securities Act” 两侧使用弯引号。官方 (c)(2) 印刷是 “If class of securities is concurrently being registered under the Securities Act”。官方 SECAUTH 印刷是 “Sec. 12, 48 Stat. 892, as amended; 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>.” 官方来源行：“[19 FR 670, Feb. 5, 1954, as amended at 59 FR 55347, Nov. 7, 1994; 62 FR 39766, July 24, 1997].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

官方 12d1-3 至 12d1-6 以及官方 12d2-2 仅点名。已经打包的 12d2-1 和已经打包的 12b-6 仅点名。

## 17 CFR 232.12：委员会的营业时间（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的 Regulation S-T Rule 13 / 17 CFR 232.13（东部时间下午 5:30 的申报日截止）保持原样。这一节是另一只时钟：官方 232.12(c) 写的是，电子申报可以在东部时间 **上午 6 点到晚上 10 点** 提交，周六、周日和 Federal holidays 除外。不要发明电子传送窗口在下午 5:30 结束。不要发明 232.12 把凡是晚上 10 点前提交的申报都视为同一营业日已提交——晚上 10 点仍算同一营业日，是官方 232.13(a)(3) / (a)(4) 对指定表格的规则，不是 232.12。不要把官方 “Eastern Daylight Saving Time” 改整齐成 “Savings”，也不要把官方 (a) / (b) / (c) 里假日的印刷改成同一种写法。不要改写已经教过的 Rule 13 框。仅供学习；没有申报人、申报代理或投资建议。

[17 CFR 232.12](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-232.12)：官方 (a) “The principal office of the Commission, at 100 F Street, NE., Washington, DC 20549, is open each day, except Saturdays, Sundays, and federal holidays, from 9 a.m. to 5:30 p.m., Eastern Standard Time or Eastern Daylight Saving Time, whichever is currently in effect, provided that hours for the filing of documents pursuant to the Acts or the rules and regulations thereunder are as set forth in paragraphs (b) and (c) of this section.” 官方 (b) “Filers may submit paper documents filed with or otherwise furnished to the Commission each day, except Saturdays, Sundays and federal holidays, from 8 a.m. to 5:30 p.m., Eastern Standard Time or Eastern Daylight Saving Time, whichever is currently in effect.” 官方 (c) “Electronic filings and other documents may be submitted to the Commission each day, except Saturdays, Sundays, and Federal holidays, from 6 a.m. to 10 p.m., Eastern Standard Time or Eastern Daylight Saving Time, whichever is currently in effect.” 官方标题印刷是 “Business hours of the Commission.” 官方印刷把 “General.” / “Submissions made in paper.” / “Submissions by direct transmission.” / “provided that” 做成斜体，前三个导语的句点也在斜体里。官方印刷是 “Eastern Daylight Saving Time”（不是 Savings）。官方 (a) 印刷是 “Saturdays, Sundays, and federal holidays”（有牛津逗号；federal 小写）。官方 (b) 印刷是 “Saturdays, Sundays and federal holidays”（没有牛津逗号；federal 小写）。官方 (c) 印刷是 “Saturdays, Sundays, and Federal holidays”（有牛津逗号；Federal 大写）。官方地址印刷是 “100 F Street, NE., Washington, DC 20549.” 官方来源行按原文把 “65 FR 24800, Apr. 27, 2000” 印了两次：“[58 FR 14670, Mar. 18, 1993; 58 FR 21349, Apr. 21, 1993, as amended at 59 FR 67761, Dec. 30, 1994; 65 FR 24800, Apr. 27, 2000; 65 FR 24800, Apr. 27, 2000; 68 FR 25799, May 13, 2003; 73 FR 32227, June 5, 2008; 86 FR 7972, Feb. 3, 2021; 88 FR 12209, Feb. 27, 2023].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经教过的 Regulation S-T Rule 13 / 17 CFR 232.13 仅点名，本框不改写它。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 Q 节（接在第 P 节 / 12b-6 之后，并仍接在 tip 的 O / 12b-15 前缀之后；Q 之后为 59/59）：

## 17 CFR 232.14：未经豁免不接受纸质申报（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 232.12，以及已经教过的 Regulation S-T Rule 13 / 17 CFR 232.13，保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：“The Commission will not accept in paper format any filing required to be submitted electronically under Rules 100 and 101 of Regulation S-T (§§ 232.100 and 232.101 respectively), unless the filing satisfies the requirements for a temporary or continuing hardship exemption under Rule 201 or 202 of Regulation S-T (§§ 232.201 or 232.202 respectively).” 官方印刷是 “§§ 232.100 and 232.101 respectively” 和 “§§ 232.201 or 232.202 respectively”——第二对用 “or，” 不是 “and。” 只点名 232.100 / 232.101 / 232.201 / 232.202；不要打包这些目录，也不要发明困境豁免引擎。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 232.12 或已经教过的 232.13。仅供学习；没有申报人、申报代理或投资建议。

232.100 / 232.101 / 232.201 / 232.202 仅点名，本框不打包这些目录。已经打包的 232.12 和已经教过的 Regulation S-T Rule 13 / 17 CFR 232.13 仅点名。

## 17 CFR 232.301：EDGAR 申报人手册（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 232.12、已经打包的 232.13 和已经打包的 232.14 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：“Filers must prepare electronic filings in the manner prescribed by the EDGAR Filer Manual, promulgated by the Commission, which sets forth the technical formatting requirements for electronic submissions. The requirements for becoming an EDGAR Filer and updating company data are set forth in the EDGAR Filer Manual, Volume I: “General Information,” Version 43 (March 2026). The requirements for filing on EDGAR are set forth in the updated EDGAR Filer Manual, Volume II: “EDGAR Filing,” Version 78 (September 2026). All EDGAR material referenced in this paragraph is incorporated by reference into this section with the approval of the Director of the Federal Register under 5 U.S.C. 552(a) and 1 CFR part 51. You must comply with these requirements in order for documents to be timely received and accepted. The EDGAR Filer Manual is made available for inspection by the Commission and the National Archives and Records Administration (NARA). The EDGAR Filer Manual may be obtained from the SEC's website at <em>https://www.sec.gov/edgar/filerinformation/current-edgar-filer-manual.</em> For information on the availability of the EDGAR Filer Manual at NARA, visit <em>www.archives.gov/federal-register/cfr/ibr-locations</em> or email <em>fr.inspection@nara.gov.</em>” 官方印刷在 “General Information” 和 “EDGAR Filing” 两侧使用弯引号。官方印刷把这些 SEC 和 NARA 地址做成斜体，包括 SEC 网址以及 <em>fr.inspection@nara.gov.</em> 斜体里的句点。官方印刷是 “You must comply with these requirements in order for documents to be timely received and accepted。” 官方标题印刷是 “EDGAR Filer Manual.” 官方来源行：“[91 FR 63478, Oct. 6, 2026].” 不要打包 Filer Manual 本身。不要把本框压进已经打包的 232.12、已经打包的 232.13，或已经打包的 232.14。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 232.301](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-232.301)：“Filers must prepare electronic filings in the manner prescribed by the EDGAR Filer Manual, promulgated by the Commission, which sets forth the technical formatting requirements for electronic submissions. ## 17 CFR 232.406：封面页 XBRL 数据标记（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经印出的 XBRL `filed` 日期框和新打包的 232.301 框保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：“Electronic filers submitting Forms 10-K (§ 249.310 of this chapter), 10-Q (§ 249.308a of this chapter), 8-K (§ 249.308 of this chapter), 20-F (§ 249.220f of this chapter) or 40-F (§ 249.240f of this chapter) who are required to submit Interactive Data Files (§ 232.11) in Inline XBRL format in accordance with this Regulation S-T must tag in Inline XBRL electronic format, in the manner provided by the EDGAR Filer Manual, all of the information provided by the electronic filer that is required on the cover page of these forms.” 官方印刷是 “or 40-F”（40-F 前面没有牛津逗号）。官方印刷是 “Interactive Data Files (§ 232.11)，” “in the manner provided by the EDGAR Filer Manual，” 以及 “all of the information provided by the electronic filer that is required on the cover page of these forms。” 官方标题印刷是 “Cover Page XBRL Data Tagging.” 官方 232.11 仅点名；不要打包那份定义目录。官方来源行：“[84 FR 12722, Apr. 2, 2019].” 不要把本框压进已经印出的 XBRL `filed` 日期框或已经打包的 232.301。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

## 17 CFR 240.12b-4：补充信息（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 12b-20 / 12b-15 / 12b-6 / 232.12 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：工作人员要求的补充信息 “shall not be required to be filed with or deemed part of the registration statement or report。” 退回请求必须 “made at the time such information is furnished to the staff，” 官方 (b) 以 “; and” 结尾，官方 (c) 引用 “the Freedom of Information Act (5 U.S.C. 552)。” 不要发明这些信息已被申报。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 12b-20 / 12b-15 / 12b-6 / 232.12。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12b-4](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-4)：“The Commission or its staff may, where it is deemed appropriate, request supplemental information concerning the registrant, a registration statement or a periodic or other report under the Act. This information shall not be required to be filed with or deemed part of the registration statement or report. The information shall be returned to the registrant upon request, provided that:” 官方 (a) “Such request is made at the time such information is furnished to the staff;” 官方 (b) “The return of such information is consistent with the protection of investors; and” 官方 (c) “The return of such information is consistent with the provisions of the Freedom of Information Act (5 U.S.C. 552).” 官方标题印刷是 “Supplemental information.” 官方来源行：“[47 FR 11465, Mar. 16, 1982].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 12b-20 / 12b-15 / 12b-6 / 232.12 仅点名，不另开核心。

## 17 CFR 240.12b-23：援引纳入（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经印出的 XBRL `filed` 日期框，以及已经打包的 12b-20 / 12b-15 / 12b-6 / 232.12，保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：官方 (a)–(e) 说明信息何时可以援引纳入。官方 (a) 允许援引纳入，“except as provided by this section or in the appropriate form。” 官方 (b) 印刷是 “U.S. Generally Accepted Accounting Principles or International Financial Reporting Standards as issued by the International Accounting Standards Board, whichever is applicable”——不要把它改整齐成光秃秃的 “GAAP / IFRS。” 官方 (d) 印刷是第二人称 “You must include an active hyperlink，” 并且在 “EDGAR” 两侧使用弯引号：Retrieval System (“EDGAR”)。官方 (e) 禁止链式援引纳入，“unless expressly permitted or required。” 官方 (a)–(e) 的斜体导语含末尾句点。不要把本规则压进已经印出的 XBRL 框，或压进已经打包的 12b-20 / 12b-15 / 12b-6 / 232.12，也不要改写 XBRL 框。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12b-23](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-23)：官方 (a) “<em>Registration statement or report.</em> Except as provided by this section or in the appropriate form, information may be incorporated by reference in answer, or partial answer, to any item of a registration statement or report.” 官方 (b) “<em>Financial information.</em> Except as provided in the Commission's rules, financial information required to be given in comparative form for two or more fiscal years or periods must not be incorporated by reference unless the information incorporated by reference includes the entire period for which the comparative data is given. In the financial statements, incorporating by reference, or cross-referencing to, information outside of the financial statements is not permitted unless otherwise specifically permitted or required by the Commission's rules or by U.S. Generally Accepted Accounting Principles or International Financial Reporting Standards as issued by the International Accounting Standards Board, whichever is applicable.” 官方 (c) “<em>Exhibits.</em> Any document or part thereof filed with the Commission pursuant to any Act administered by the Commission may be incorporated by reference as an exhibit to any statement or report filed with the Commission by the same or any other person. Any document or part thereof filed with an exchange pursuant to the Act may be incorporated by reference as an exhibit to any statement or report filed with the exchange by the same or any other person. If any modification has occurred in the text of any document incorporated by reference since the filing thereof, the registrant must file with the reference a statement containing the text of any such modification and the date thereof.” 官方 (d) “<em>Hyperlinks.</em> You must include an active hyperlink to information incorporated into a registration statement or report by reference if such information is publicly available on the Commission's Electronic Data Gathering, Analysis and Retrieval System (“EDGAR”) at the time the registration statement or form is filed. For hyperlinking to exhibits, please refer to Item 601 of Regulation S-K (§ 229.601 of this chapter) or the appropriate form.” 官方 (e) “<em>General.</em> Include an express statement clearly describing the specific location of the information you are incorporating by reference. The statement must identify the document where the information was originally filed or submitted and the location of the information within that document. The statement must be made at the particular place where the information is required, if applicable. Information must not be incorporated by reference in any case where such incorporation would render the disclosure incomplete, unclear, or confusing. For example, unless expressly permitted or required, disclosure must not be incorporated by reference from a second document if that second document incorporates information pertinent to such disclosure by reference to a third document.” 官方标题印刷是 “Incorporation by reference.” 官方来源行：“[84 FR 12727, Apr. 2, 2019].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经印出的 XBRL `filed` 日期框，以及已经打包的 12b-20 / 12b-15 / 12b-6 / 232.12，仅点名；本框不改写 XBRL 框。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 S 节（接在第 R 节 / 12b-4 之后；S 之后为 68/68）：

```text
=== S. 17 CFR 240.12b-23: Incorporation by reference ===
official caption typography is Incorporation by reference.
12b-23(a): Registration statement or report. Except as provided by this section or in the appropriate form, information may be incorporated by reference in answer, or partial answer, to any item of a registration statement or report.
12b-23(b): Financial information. Except as provided in the Commission's rules, financial information required to be given in comparative form for two or more fiscal years or periods must not be incorporated by reference unless the information incorporated by reference includes the entire period for which the comparative data is given. In the financial statements, incorporating by reference, or cross-referencing to, information outside of the financial statements is not permitted unless otherwise specifically permitted or required by the Commission's rules or by U.S. Generally Accepted Accounting Principles or International Financial Reporting Standards as issued by the International Accounting Standards Board, whichever is applicable.
12b-23(c): Exhibits. Any document or part thereof filed with the Commission pursuant to any Act administered by the Commission may be incorporated by reference as an exhibit to any statement or report filed with the Commission by the same or any other person. Any document or part thereof filed with an exchange pursuant to the Act may be incorporated by reference as an exhibit to any statement or report filed with the exchange by the same or any other person. If any modification has occurred in the text of any document incorporated by reference since the filing thereof, the registrant must file with the reference a statement containing the text of any such modification and the date thereof.
12b-23(d): Hyperlinks. You must include an active hyperlink to information incorporated into a registration statement or report by reference if such information is publicly available on the Commission's Electronic Data Gathering, Analysis and Retrieval System (“EDGAR”) at the time the registration statement or form is filed. For hyperlinking to exhibits, please refer to Item 601 of Regulation S-K (§ 229.601 of this chapter) or the appropriate form.
12b-23(e): General. Include an express statement clearly describing the specific location of the information you are incorporating by reference. The statement must identify the document where the information was originally filed or submitted and the location of the information within that document. The statement must be made at the particular place where the information is required, if applicable. Information must not be incorporated by reference in any case where such incorporation would render the disclosure incomplete, unclear, or confusing. For example, unless expressly permitted or required, disclosure must not be incorporated by reference from a second document if that second document incorporates information pertinent to such disclosure by reference to a third document.

official (e) typography is "unless expressly permitted or required."
```

## 17 CFR 240.13a-11：Form 8-K 临时报告（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的 Form 8-K 项目时钟 / Item 5.07、已经教过的 Form 6-K promptly 提交时钟，以及已经打包的 14e-8，保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：官方 13a-11(a) 要求就 Form 8-K “within the period specified in that form unless substantially the same information as that required by Form 8-K has been previously reported by the registrant” 提交临时报告。不要在 13a-11 本身里发明数字时钟。官方 (b) 的例外包括按 13a-16 在 Form 6-K 上报告的外国私人发行人——已经教过的 6-K 时钟只点名——以及 ADR 发行人、外国政府和须按 § 270.30a-1 申报的投资公司，“except where such an investment company is required to file notice of a blackout period pursuant to § 245.104 of this chapter。” 官方 (c) 的项目清单必须按原文抄写：“Item 1.01, 1.02, 1.05, 2.03, 2.04, 2.05, 2.06, 4.02(a), 5.02(e), or 6.03。” 不要往这一除外清单里发明 Item 2.02、Item 5.07，或光秃秃的 4.02 / 5.02。官方印刷是：这类未申报 “shall be deemed to be a violation of 15 U.S.C. 78j(b) and § 240.10b-5” 并不成立。官方 13a-1 只点名，不要把它当作第二个核心来打包。不要把本规则压进已经教过的 Form 8-K 项目时钟 / Item 5.07 / 已经打包的 14e-8。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.13a-11](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13a-11)：官方 (a) “Except as provided in paragraph (b) of this section, every registrant subject to § 240.13a-1 shall file a current report on Form 8-K within the period specified in that form unless substantially the same information as that required by Form 8-K has been previously reported by the registrant.” 官方 (b) “This section shall not apply to foreign governments, foreign private issuers required to make reports on Form 6-K (§ 249.306 of this chapter) pursuant to § 240.13a-16, issuers of American Depositary Receipts for securities of any foreign issuer, or investment companies required to file reports pursuant to § 270.30a-1 of this chapter under the Investment Company Act of 1940, except where such an investment company is required to file notice of a blackout period pursuant to § 245.104 of this chapter.” 官方 (c) “No failure to file a report on Form 8-K that is required solely pursuant to Item 1.01, 1.02, 1.05, 2.03, 2.04, 2.05, 2.06, 4.02(a), 5.02(e), or 6.03 of Form 8-K shall be deemed to be a violation of 15 U.S.C. 78j(b) and § 240.10b-5.” 官方标题印刷是 “Current reports on Form 8-K (§ 249.308 of this chapter).” 官方来源行：“[42 FR 4428, Jan. 25, 1977, as amended at 50 FR 27939, July 9, 1985; 68 FR 4355, Jan. 28, 2003; 69 FR 15618, Mar. 25, 2004; 70 FR 1621, Jan. 7, 2005; 71 FR 53260, Sept. 8, 2006; 75 FR 56780, Sept. 16, 2010; 81 FR 82020, Nov. 18, 2016; 88 FR 51493, Aug. 4, 2023; 90 FR 9689, Feb. 18, 2025].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

官方 13a-1 仅点名，本框不把它当作第二个核心来打包。已经教过的 Form 8-K 项目时钟 / Item 5.07、已经教过的 Form 6-K promptly 提交时钟，以及已经打包的 14e-8，仅点名。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 T 节（接在第 S 节 / 12b-23 之后；T 之后为 72/72）：

## 17 CFR 240.12b-10：对适当表格的要求（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 12b-15 / 12b-25 / 12b-4 / 12b-23 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：每份报表或报告 “shall be on the form prescribed therefor by the Commission, as in effect on the date of filing。” 任何报表或报告 “shall be deemed to be filed on the proper form unless objection to the form is made by the Commission within thirty days after the date of filing。” 不要把 “therefor” 改整齐成 “therefore，” 也不要把 “thirty days” 改整齐成 “30 days。” 不要把本规则压进已经打包的 12b-15 / 12b-25 / 12b-4 / 12b-23。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12b-10](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-10)：“Every statement or report shall be on the form prescribed therefor by the Commission, as in effect on the date of filing. Any statement or report shall be deemed to be filed on the proper form unless objection to the form is made by the Commission within thirty days after the date of filing.” 官方标题印刷是 “Requirements as to proper form.” 官方来源行：“[30 FR 2022, Feb. 13, 1965].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 12b-15 / 12b-25 / 12b-4 / 12b-23 仅点名。

## 17 CFR 240.12b-21：未知或不可得的信息（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 12b-20 / 12b-23 / 12b-4 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：所需信息 “need be given only insofar as it is known or reasonably available to the registrant。” 官方标题印刷是 “Information unknown or not available。”——标题并不写 “reasonably，” 尽管正文开句用了 “known or reasonably available。” 官方 (a) 仍要求给出登记人已经掌握、或可以不经不合理努力或费用取得的信息，并且要 “together with the sources thereof。” 官方 (b) 要求一份说明，要么表明会涉及不合理努力或费用，要么表明与掌握该信息的人没有关联，并写明向该人请求信息的结果。官方 API XML 没有 CITA 元素；不要发明来源行。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 12b-20 / 12b-23 / 12b-4。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12b-21](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-21)：开句 “Information required need be given only insofar as it is known or reasonably available to the registrant. If any required information is unknown and not reasonably available to the registrant, either because the obtaining thereof would involve unreasonable effort or expense, or because it rests peculiarly within the knowledge of another person not affiliated with the registrant, the information may be omitted, subject to the following conditions.” 官方 (a) “The registrant shall give such information on the subject as it possesses or can acquire without unreasonable effort or expense, together with the sources thereof.” 官方 (b) “The registrant shall include a statement either showing that unreasonable effort or expense would be involved or indicating the absence of any affiliation with the person within whose knowledge the information rests and stating the result of a request made to such person for the information.” 官方标题印刷是 “Information unknown or not available.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA 元素；本课不发明来源行。 Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 12b-20 / 12b-23 / 12b-4 仅点名。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 V 节（接在第 U 节 / 12b-10 之后；V 之后为 79/79）：

```text
12b-21(b): The registrant shall include a statement either showing that unreasonable effort or expense would be involved or indicating the absence of any affiliation with the person within whose knowledge the information rests and stating the result of a request made to such person for the information.
```

## 17 CFR 240.12b-22：控制权免责声明（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 12b-2 / 12b-20 / 12b-21 / 12b-23 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：“If the existence of control is open to reasonable doubt in any instance, the registrant may disclaim the existence of control and any admission thereof; in such case, however, the registrant shall state the material facts pertinent to the possible existence of control.” 官方印刷是 “open to reasonable doubt，” “any admission thereof，” “in such case, however，” 以及 “material facts pertinent to the possible existence of control.” 官方印刷在 “thereof” 后面保留分号。免责声明并不是不存在控制权的认定。官方标题印刷是 “Disclaimer of control.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA 元素；不要发明来源行。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 12b-2 / 12b-20 / 12b-21 / 12b-23。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.12b-22](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-22)：“If the existence of control is open to reasonable doubt in any instance, the registrant may disclaim the existence of control and any admission thereof; in such case, however, the registrant shall state the material facts pertinent to the possible existence of control.” 官方标题印刷是 “Disclaimer of control.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA 元素；本课不发明来源行。 Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

## 17 CFR 240.13a-1：年度报告要求（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的 Form 10-K 时钟、已经打包的 13a-11，以及已经打包的 13a-14，保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：就《交易法》第 12 条注册的发行人，须 “for each fiscal year after the last full fiscal year for which financial statements were filed in its registration statement” 提交年度报告。官方印刷是 “Annual reports shall be filed within the period specified in the appropriate form。” 不要在 13a-1 本身里发明数字时钟。官方印刷是 “section 12 of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>)，” 字母 l 按官方斜体处理；不要把这个斜体 l 改整齐成普通 “78l” 或 “781。” 课文里用 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em> 表示官方斜体。官方 15d-1 只点名——官方 15d-1 标题印刷是单数 “Requirement of annual reports。”——不要把它当作第二个核心来打包。不要把本规则压进已经教过的 Form 10-K 时钟、已经打包的 13a-11，或已经打包的 13a-14。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.13a-1](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13a-1)：“Every issuer having securities registered pursuant to section 12 of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>) shall file an annual report on the appropriate form authorized or prescribed therefor for each fiscal year after the last full fiscal year for which financial statements were filed in its registration statement. Annual reports shall be filed within the period specified in the appropriate form.” 官方标题印刷是 “Requirements of annual reports.” 官方来源行：“[62 FR 39767, July 24, 1997].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

官方 15d-1 仅点名，本框不把它当作第二个核心来打包。已经教过的 Form 10-K 时钟、已经打包的 13a-11，以及已经打包的 13a-14，仅点名。

## 17 CFR 240.13a-13：Form 10-Q 季度报告（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的 Form 10-Q 时钟、已经打包的 13a-11，以及新的 13a-1 框，保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：官方 13a-13(a) 要求就 Form 10-Q（§ 249.308a of this chapter，不是 § 249.308）“within the period specified in General Instruction A.1. to that form” 为 “each of the first three quarters of each fiscal year of the issuer” 提交季度报告。不要发明第四个财政季度的 10-Q。规则里唯一的数字时钟是首次报告的替代：“within 45 days after the effective date of the registration statement or on or before the date on which such report would have been required to be filed if the issuer has been required to file reports on Form 10-Q as of its last fiscal quarter, whichever is later。” 官方印刷是 “has been required，” 不是 “had been required。” 官方 (b) 的例外包括须按 § 270.30a-1 申报的投资公司、须按 § 240.13a-16 申报的外国私人发行人，以及须按 § 240.13a-17 申报的资产支持发行人——已经教过的 6-K 时钟和尚未打包的 13a-17 只点名；不要用官方 (c) 去搭一套资产支持或矿业公司引擎。官方 (d) 印刷是：Part I 财务信息 shall not be deemed to be “filed” for the purpose of Section 18 of the Act，但 shall be subject to all other provisions of the Act；“filed” 两侧使用弯引号 U+201C / U+201D。官方 15d-13 / 13a-17 只点名，不要发明 15d-13 的表格编号，也不要把它们当作第二个核心来打包。不要把本规则压进已经教过的 Form 10-Q 时钟、已经打包的 13a-11，或新的 13a-1 框。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.13a-13](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13a-13)：官方 (a) “Except as provided in paragraphs (b) and (c) of this section, every issuer that has securities registered pursuant to section 12 of the Act and is required to file annual reports pursuant to section 13 of the Act, and has filed or intends to file such reports on Form 10-K (§ 249.310 of this chapter), shall file a quarterly report on Form 10-Q (§ 249.308a of this chapter) within the period specified in General Instruction A.1. to that form for each of the first three quarters of each fiscal year of the issuer, commencing with the first fiscal quarter following the most recent fiscal year for which full financial statements were included in the registration statement, or, if the registration statement included financial statements for an interim period subsequent to the most recent fiscal year end meeting the requirements of Article 10 of Regulation S-X and Rule 8-03 of Regulation S-X for smaller reporting companies, for the first fiscal quarter subsequent to the quarter reported upon in the registration statement. The first quarterly report of the issuer shall be filed either within 45 days after the effective date of the registration statement or on or before the date on which such report would have been required to be filed if the issuer has been required to file reports on Form 10-Q as of its last fiscal quarter, whichever is later.” 官方 (b) “The provisions of this rule shall not apply to the following issuers:” 官方 (b)(1) “Investment companies required to file reports pursuant to § 270.30a-1;” 官方 (b)(2) “Foreign private issuers required to file reports pursuant to § 240.13a-16; and” 官方 (b)(3) “Asset-backed issuers required to file reports pursuant to § 240.13a-17.” 官方 (c) “Part I of the quarterly reports on Form 10-Q need not be filed by:” 官方 (c)(1) “Mutual life insurance companies; or” 官方 (c)(2) “Mining companies not in the production stage but engaged primarily in the exploration for the development of mineral deposits other than oil, gas or coal, if all of the following conditions are met:” 官方 (c)(2)(i) “The registrant has not been in production during the current fiscal year or the two years immediately prior thereto; except that being in production for an aggregate period of not more than eight months over the three-year period shall not be a violation of this condition.” 官方 (c)(2)(ii) “Receipts from the sale of mineral products or from the operations of mineral producing properties by the registrant and its subsidiaries combined have not exceeded $500,000 in any of the most recent six years and have not aggregated more than $1,500,000 in the most recent six fiscal years.” 官方 (d) “Notwithstanding the foregoing provisions of this section, the financial information required by Part I of Form 10-Q shall not be deemed to be “filed” for the purpose of Section 18 of the Act or otherwise subject to the liabilities of that section of the Act, but shall be subject to all other provisions of the Act.” 官方标题印刷是 “Quarterly reports on Form 10-Q (§ 249.308a of this chapter).” 官方来源行：“[42 FR 24064, May 12, 1977, as amended at 48 FR 19877, May 3, 1983; 50 FR 27939, July 9, 1985; 54 FR 10317, Mar. 13, 1989; 57 FR 10615, Mar. 27, 1992; 61 FR 30403, June 14, 1996; 70 FR 1621, Jan. 7, 2005; 73 FR 975, Jan. 4, 2008; 81 FR 82020, Nov. 18, 2016].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

官方 15d-13 / 13a-17 仅点名，本框不把它们当作第二个核心来打包。已经教过的 Form 10-Q 时钟、已经打包的 13a-11、新的 13a-1 框，以及已经教过的 Form 6-K promptly 提交时钟，仅点名。

```text
=== X. 17 CFR 240.13a-13: Quarterly reports on Form 10-Q ===
official caption typography is Quarterly reports on Form 10-Q (§ 249.308a of this chapter).
13a-13(a): Except as provided in paragraphs (b) and (c) of this section, every issuer that has securities registered pursuant to section 12 of the Act and is required to file annual reports pursuant to section 13 of the Act, and has filed or intends to file such reports on Form 10-K (§ 249.310 of this chapter), shall file a quarterly report on Form 10-Q (§ 249.308a of this chapter) within the period specified in General Instruction A.1. to that form for each of the first three quarters of each fiscal year of the issuer, commencing with the first fiscal quarter following the most recent fiscal year for which full financial statements were included in the registration statement, or, if the registration statement included financial statements for an interim period subsequent to the most recent fiscal year end meeting the requirements of Article 10 of Regulation S-X and Rule 8-03 of Regulation S-X for smaller reporting companies, for the first fiscal quarter subsequent to the quarter reported upon in the registration statement. The first quarterly report of the issuer shall be filed either within 45 days after the effective date of the registration statement or on or before the date on which such report would have been required to be filed if the issuer has been required to file reports on Form 10-Q as of its last fiscal quarter, whichever is later.
13a-13(b): The provisions of this rule shall not apply to the following issuers:
13a-13(b)(1): Investment companies required to file reports pursuant to § 270.30a-1;
13a-13(b)(2): Foreign private issuers required to file reports pursuant to § 240.13a-16; and
13a-13(b)(3): Asset-backed issuers required to file reports pursuant to § 240.13a-17.

13a-13(c)(2)(ii): Receipts from the sale of mineral products or from the operations of mineral producing properties by the registrant and its subsidiaries combined have not exceeded $500,000 in any of the most recent six years and have not aggregated more than $1,500,000 in the most recent six fiscal years.
13a-13(d): Notwithstanding the foregoing provisions of this section, the financial information required by Part I of Form 10-Q shall not be deemed to be “filed” for the purpose of Section 18 of the Act or otherwise subject to the liabilities of that section of the Act, but shall be subject to all other provisions of the Act.
```

## 17 CFR 240.13a-17：资产支持证券发行人的 Form 10-D 报告（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 13a-13 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：凡受 § 240.13a-1 约束的资产支持证券发行人，应当在 Form 10-D（§ 249.312 of this chapter）上提交报告。这类报告应当 “within the period specified in Form 10-D.” 不要发明一个数字形式的 Form 10-D 时钟。官方标题印刷是 “Reports of asset-backed issuers on Form 10-D (§ 249.312 of this chapter).” 官方表格引用是 § 249.312，不是已经打包的 13a-13 的 § 249.308a，也不是已经打包的 15d-13 的 § 249.308。官方来源行：“[70 FR 1621, Jan. 7, 2005].” 只点名 Form 10-D / § 249.312；不要打包该表格说明书目录，也不要改写已经打包的 13a-13 框。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

## 17 CFR 243.102：对反欺诈责任没有影响（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的 Regulation FD 时钟写在 `backtest-traps`（243.100–101），保持原样，不要改写那个框。已经打包的 10b-5、已经打包的 13a-11(c)，以及只被点名的 244.102，也保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：“No failure to make a public disclosure required solely by § 243.100 shall be deemed to be a violation of Rule 10b-5 (17 CFR 240.10b-5) under the Securities Exchange Act。” 不要把这一否定说反。官方印刷是 “required solely by § 243.100，” 并且同时写出 “Rule 10b-5” 和 “17 CFR 240.10b-5”；这句并不写 “of 1934。” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA 元素；不要发明来源行。不要把本规则压进已经教过的 Regulation FD 时钟、已经打包的 10b-5、已经打包的 13a-11(c)，或只被点名的 244.102。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

## 17 CFR 243.103：对交易所法申报状态没有影响（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 243.102、已经教过的写在 `backtest-traps` 的 Regulation FD 时钟、已经打包的 10b-5，以及只被点名的 244.102，保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：“A failure to make a public disclosure required solely by § 243.100 shall not affect whether:” 官方 (a) 是发行人是否被视为已按 Section 13 or 15(d) 提交全部须提交的材料，“or where applicable, has made those filings in a timely manner，” 或官方 (b) 是否存在 Rule 144(c) 目的下的 adequate current public information。不要把 “shall not affect” 说反。官方印刷是 “239.16b，” 不是 “239.16。” 官方印刷把 “et seq.” 做成斜体。官方印刷是 “or where applicable, has made those filings in a timely manner”——保留 “applicable” 后面的逗号。官方 (b) 印刷是 “adequate current public information about the issuer for purposes of § 230.144(c) of this chapter (Rule 144(c)).” 只点名这些表格和 Rule 144(c)；不要去做这些表格引擎。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 243.102、已经教过的 Regulation FD 时钟、已经打包的 10b-5，或只被点名的 244.102，也不要改写 FD 框。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 243.103](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-243.103)：官方开句 “A failure to make a public disclosure required solely by § 243.100 shall not affect whether:” 官方 (a) “For purposes of Forms S-3 (17 CFR 239.13), S-8 (17 CFR 239.16b) and SF-3 (17 CFR 239.45) under the Securities Act of 1933 (15 U.S.C. 77a <em>et seq.</em>), or Form N-2 (17 CFR 239.14 and 274.11a-1) under the Securities Act of 1933 (15 U.S.C. 77a <em>et seq.</em>) and the Investment Company Act of 1940 (15 U.S.C. 80a-1 <em>et seq.</em>), an issuer is deemed to have filed all the material required to be filed pursuant to Section 13 or 15(d) of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934 (15 U.S.C. 78m or 78o(d)) or where applicable, has made those filings in a timely manner; or” 官方 (b) “There is adequate current public information about the issuer for purposes of § 230.144(c) of this chapter (Rule 144(c)).” 官方标题印刷是 “No effect on Exchange Act reporting status.” 官方来源行：“[65 FR 51738, Aug. 24, 2000, as amended at 79 FR 57344, Sept. 24, 2014; 85 FR 33360, June 1, 2020].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

这些表格和 Rule 144(c) 仅点名。已经打包的 243.102、已经教过的 Regulation FD 时钟、已经打包的 10b-5，以及只被点名的 244.102，仅点名。

## 17 CFR 240.15d-1：年度报告要求（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 13a-1 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：Every registrant under the Securities Act of 1933 shall file an annual report “on the appropriate form authorized or prescribed therefor，” for the fiscal year in which the registration statement became effective and for each fiscal year thereafter, unless exempt by section 15(d) of the Act or rules thereunder。官方印刷是 “Annual reports shall be filed within the period specified in the appropriate report form.” 不要发明数字时钟，也不要把这句改整齐成已经打包的 13a-1 的 “the appropriate form。” 官方印刷是 “therefor”；不要改整齐成 “therefore。” 官方标题印刷是单数 “Requirement of annual reports.”——已经打包的 13a-1 是复数 “Requirements of annual reports.” 不要改写已经打包的 13a-1 框，也不要把标题或 CITA 改整齐进 13a-1。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.15d-1](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.15d-1)：“Every registrant under the Securities Act of 1933 shall file an annual report, on the appropriate form authorized or prescribed therefor, for the fiscal year in which the registration statement under the Securities Act of 1933 became effective and for each fiscal year thereafter, unless the registrant is exempt from such filing by section 15(d) of the Act or rules thereunder. Annual reports shall be filed within the period specified in the appropriate report form.” 官方标题印刷是 “Requirement of annual reports.” 官方来源行：“[47 FR 17052, Apr. 21, 1982, as amended at 61 FR 49960, Sept. 24, 1996].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 13a-1 仅点名，本框不改写它。

## 17 CFR 240.15d-11：Form 8-K 临时报告（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 13a-11 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：官方 15d-11(a) 要求 “every registrant subject to § 240.15d-1” 在 “within the period specified in that form” 提交 Form 8-K 临时报告，除非实质上相同的信息此前已经 reported。不要在 15d-11 本身里发明数字时钟。官方 (b) 点名的是 § 240.15d-16 和 Form 6-K（§ 249.306 of this chapter），不是已经打包的 13a-11 的 § 240.13a-16。官方 (c) 印刷是 “No failure to file a report on Form 8-K that is required solely pursuant to Item 1.01, 1.02, 1.05, 2.03, 2.04, 2.05, 2.06, 4.02(a), 5.02(e), or 6.03 of Form 8-K shall be deemed to be a violation of 15 U.S.C. 78j(b) and § 240.10b-5.” 按原文打包官方 (c)；不要把这一否定说反。不要把官方 CITA 改整齐进已经打包的 13a-11。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.15d-11](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.15d-11)：官方 (a) “Except as provided in paragraph (b) of this section, every registrant subject to § 240.15d-1 shall file a current report on Form 8-K within the period specified in that form unless substantially the same information as that required by Form 8-K has been previously reported by the registrant.” 官方 (b) “This section shall not apply to foreign governments, foreign private issuers required to make reports on Form 6-K (§ 249.306 of this chapter) pursuant to § 240.15d-16, issuers of American Depositary Receipts for securities of any foreign issuer, or investment companies required to file reports pursuant to § 270.30a-1 of this chapter under the Investment Company Act of 1940, except where such an investment company is required to file notice of a blackout period pursuant to § 245.104 of this chapter.” 官方 (c) “No failure to file a report on Form 8-K that is required solely pursuant to Item 1.01, 1.02, 1.05, 2.03, 2.04, 2.05, 2.06, 4.02(a), 5.02(e), or 6.03 of Form 8-K shall be deemed to be a violation of 15 U.S.C. 78j(b) and § 240.10b-5.” 官方标题印刷是 “Current reports on Form 8-K (§ 249.308 of this chapter).” 官方来源行：“[42 FR 4429, Jan. 25, 1977, as amended at 50 FR 27939, July 9, 1985; 68 FR 4355, Jan. 28, 2002; 69 FR 15618, Mar. 25, 2004; 70 FR 1622, Jan. 7, 2005; 71 FR 53263, Sept. 8, 2006; 75 FR 56792, Sept. 16, 2010; 81 FR 82020, Nov. 18, 2016; 88 FR 51943, Aug. 4, 2023; 90 FR 9690, Feb. 18, 2025].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 13a-11 仅点名，本框不把标题或 CITA 改整齐进 13a-11。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 AC 节（接在第 AB 节 / 15d-1 之后；AC 之后为 104/104）：

```text
=== AC. 17 CFR 240.15d-11: Current reports on Form 8-K ===
```

## 17 CFR 240.15d-13：Form 10-Q 季度报告（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 13a-13 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：官方 15d-13(a) 要求在 Form 10-Q（§ 249.308 of this chapter，不是已经打包的 13a-13 的 § 249.308a）上，“within the period specified in General Instruction A.1 to that form”，为 “each of the first three quarters of each fiscal year of the issuer” 提交季度报告。不要发明四个财政季度都交 10-Q。官方印刷在 “A.1” 后面没有句点。官方印刷是 “had been required，” 不是已经打包的 13a-13 的 “has been required。” 官方 (b) 点名的是 15d-16 / 15d-17，不是 13a-16 / 13a-17。官方 (d) 印刷是小写 “section 18，” 不是已经打包的 13a-13 的 “Section 18。” 官方 (d) 和官方 (e) 在 “filed” 两侧使用弯引号 U+201C / U+201D。按原文打包官方 (e)，包括 “or financial information submitted in lieu thereof pursuant to paragraph (d) of this section”；不要丢掉官方 (e)，也不要发明官方 (d) 授权替代申报。54 FR 10319 与 61 FR 30403 之间的官方印刷是逗号，不是分号。不要把这条 CITA 或表格引用改整齐进已经打包的 13a-13。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.15d-13](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.15d-13)：官方 (a) “Except as provided in paragraphs (b) and (c) of this section, every issuer that has securities registered pursuant to the Securities Act and is required to file annual reports pursuant to section 15(d) of the Act on Form 10-K (§ 249.310 of this chapter) shall file a quarterly report on Form 10-Q (§ 249.308 of this chapter) within the period specified in General Instruction A.1 to that form for each of the first three quarters of each fiscal year of the issuer, commencing with the first fiscal quarter following the most recent fiscal year for which full financial statements were included in the registration statement, or, if the registration statement included financial statements for an interim period after the most recent fiscal year end meeting the requirements of Article 10 of Regulation S-X, or Rule 8-03 of Regulation S-X for smaller reporting companies, for the first fiscal quarter after the quarter reported upon in the registration statement. The first quarterly report of the issuer shall be filed either within 45 days after the effective date of the registration statement or on or before the date on which such report would have been required to be filed if the issuer had been required to file reports on Form 10-Q as of its last fiscal quarter, whichever is later.” 官方 (b) “The provisions of this rule shall not apply to the following issuers:” 官方 (b)(1) “Investment companies required to file reports pursuant to § 270.30a-1;” 官方 (b)(2) “Foreign private issuers required to file reports pursuant to § 240.15d-16; and” 官方 (b)(3) “Asset-backed issuers required to file reports pursuant to § 240.15d-17.” 官方 (c) “Part I of the quarterly reports on Form 10-Q need not be filed by:” 官方 (c)(1) “Mutual life insurance companies; or” 官方 (c)(2) “Mining companies not in the production stage but engaged primarily in the exploration for the development of mineral deposits other than oil, gas or coal, if all of the following conditions are met:” 官方 (c)(2)(i) “The registrant has not been in production during the current fiscal year or the two years immediately prior thereto; except that being in production for an aggregate period of not more than eight months over the three-year period shall not be a violation of this condition.” 官方 (c)(2)(ii) “Receipts from the sale of mineral products or from the operations of mineral producing properties by the registrant and its subsidiaries combined have not exceeded $500,000 in any of the most recent six years and have not aggregated more than $1,500,000 in the most recent six fiscal years.” 官方 (d) “Notwithstanding the foregoing provisions of this section, the financial information required by Part I of Form 10-Q shall not be deemed to be “filed” for the purpose of section 18 of the Act or otherwise subject to the liabilities of that section of the Act, but shall be subject to all other provisions of the Act.” 官方 (e) “Notwithstanding the foregoing provisions of this section, the financial information required by Part I of Form 10-Q, or financial information submitted in lieu thereof pursuant to paragraph (d) of this section, shall not be deemed to be “filed” for the purpose of section 18 of the Act or otherwise subject to the liabilities of that section of the Act, but shall be subject to all other provisions of the Act.” 官方标题印刷是 “Quarterly reports on Form 10-Q (§ 249.308 of this chapter).” 官方来源行：“[42 FR 24065, May 12, 1977, as amended at 46 FR 63255, Dec. 31, 1981; 50 FR 27939, July 9, 1985; 54 FR 10319, Mar. 13, 1989, 61 FR 30403, June 14, 1996; 70 FR 1622, Jan. 7, 2005; 73 FR 978, Jan. 4, 2008; 81 FR 82020, Nov. 18, 2016].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 13a-13 仅点名，本框不把官方 CITA 或表格引用改整齐进 13a-13。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 AD 节（接在第 AC 节 / 15d-11 之后；AD 之后为 110/110）：

```text
=== AD. 17 CFR 240.15d-13: Quarterly reports on Form 10-Q ===
official caption typography is Quarterly reports on Form 10-Q (§ 249.308 of this chapter).
15d-13(a): Except as provided in paragraphs (b) and (c) of this section, every issuer that has securities registered pursuant to the Securities Act and is required to file annual reports pursuant to section 15(d) of the Act on Form 10-K (§ 249.310 of this chapter) shall file a quarterly report on Form 10-Q (§ 249.308 of this chapter) within the period specified in General Instruction A.1 to that form for each of the first three quarters of each fiscal year of the issuer, commencing with the first fiscal quarter following the most recent fiscal year for which full financial statements were included in the registration statement, or, if the registration statement included financial statements for an interim period after the most recent fiscal year end meeting the requirements of Article 10 of Regulation S-X, or Rule 8-03 of Regulation S-X for smaller reporting companies, for the first fiscal quarter after the quarter reported upon in the registration statement. The first quarterly report of the issuer shall be filed either within 45 days after the effective date of the registration statement or on or before the date on which such report would have been required to be filed if the issuer had been required to file reports on Form 10-Q as of its last fiscal quarter, whichever is later.
15d-13(b): The provisions of this rule shall not apply to the following issuers:
15d-13(b)(1): Investment companies required to file reports pursuant to § 270.30a-1;
15d-13(b)(2): Foreign private issuers required to file reports pursuant to § 240.15d-16; and
15d-13(b)(3): Asset-backed issuers required to file reports pursuant to § 240.15d-17.
15d-13(c): Part I of the quarterly reports on Form 10-Q need not be filed by:
15d-13(c)(1): Mutual life insurance companies; or
15d-13(c)(2): Mining companies not in the production stage but engaged primarily in the exploration for the development of mineral deposits other than oil, gas or coal, if all of the following conditions are met:
15d-13(c)(2)(i): The registrant has not been in production during the current fiscal year or the two years immediately prior thereto; except that being in production for an aggregate period of not more than eight months over the three-year period shall not be a violation of this condition.
15d-13(c)(2)(ii): Receipts from the sale of mineral products or from the operations of mineral producing properties by the registrant and its subsidiaries combined have not exceeded $500,000 in any of the most recent six years and have not aggregated more than $1,500,000 in the most recent six fiscal years.
15d-13(d): Notwithstanding the foregoing provisions of this section, the financial information required by Part I of Form 10-Q shall not be deemed to be “filed” for the purpose of section 18 of the Act or otherwise subject to the liabilities of that section of the Act, but shall be subject to all other provisions of the Act.
```

## 17 CFR 240.15d-17：资产支持证券发行人的 Form 10-D 报告（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 15d-13 保持原样。新打包的 13a-17 框也保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：凡受 § 240.15d-1 约束的资产支持证券发行人，应当在 Form 10-D（§ 249.312 of this chapter）上提交报告。官方触发印刷是 § 240.15d-1，不是 § 240.13a-1。这类报告应当 “within the period specified in Form 10-D.” 官方标题印刷与 13a-17 用词相同：“Reports of asset-backed issuers on Form 10-D (§ 249.312 of this chapter).” 官方来源行：“[70 FR 1622, Jan. 7, 2005].” 不要把这一页码改整齐成 13a-17 的 70 FR 1621，也不要把本框压进已经打包的 15d-13 或新打包的 13a-17 框。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.15d-17](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.15d-17)：“Every asset-backed issuer subject to § 240.15d-1 shall make reports on Form 10-D (§ 249.312 of this chapter). Such reports shall be filed within the period specified in Form 10-D.” 官方标题印刷是 “Reports of asset-backed issuers on Form 10-D (§ 249.312 of this chapter).” 官方来源行：“[70 FR 1622, Jan. 7, 2005].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 15d-13 仅点名，本框不压进该框。新打包的 13a-17 框仅点名；官方触发是 § 240.15d-1，不是 § 240.13a-1。
