## 17 CFR 240.15d-6：暂停申报义务（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 12g-4 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：如果按 section 15(d) of the Act 就某一财政年度暂停申报义务，发行人应当 “within 30 days after the beginning of the first fiscal year，” “file a notice on Form 15 informing the Commission of such suspension”，“unless Form 15 has already been filed pursuant to Rule 12h-3。” 官方对继受发行人的印刷是 “the issuer's merger into, or consolidation with, another issuer or issuers”，以及 “the notice shall be filed by the successor issuer.” 官方标题印刷是 “Suspension of duty to file reports.” 如果点名 Rule 12h-3，其官方标题印刷是 “Suspension of duty to file reports under section 15(d).”——不要把这两个标题改整齐成同一个。官方 SECAUTH 印刷把 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>(g)(4), 78<em>l</em>(h) 里的字母 l 做成斜体；面向学员的印刷是 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>(g)(4), 78<em>l</em>(h)。只点名 12h-3；不要打包该目录。这一框是 Form 15 通知时钟，不是制定法 section 15(d) 的暂停测试，也不是已经打包的 12g-4。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.15d-6](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.15d-6)：“If the duty of an issuer to file reports pursuant to section 15(d) of the Act as to any fiscal year is suspended as provided in section 15(d) of the Act, such issuer shall, within 30 days after the beginning of the first fiscal year, file a notice on Form 15 informing the Commission of such suspension unless Form 15 has already been filed pursuant to Rule 12h-3. If the suspension resulted from the issuer's merger into, or consolidation with, another issuer or issuers, the notice shall be filed by the successor issuer.” 官方标题印刷是 “Suspension of duty to file reports.” 官方 SECAUTH 印刷是 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>(g)(4), 78<em>l</em>(h)，字母 l 按官方斜体。官方来源行：“[49 FR 12690, Mar. 30, 1984].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

官方 12h-3 仅点名，本框不打包该目录。已经打包的 12g-4 仅点名。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 AE 节（接在第 AD 节 / 15d-13 之后；AE 之后为 113/113）：

If the suspension resulted from the issuer's merger into, or consolidation with, another issuer or issuers, the notice shall be filed by the successor issuer.

## SEC 的 XBRL 数据：`filed` 日期才是“已知”日期（2026-11-11 核实）

**免费、官方的基本面来源。** 公司在 10-Q、10-K、8-K 等申报里用 XBRL（一种机器可读格式）标注的财务数字，SEC 在 `data.sec.gov` 上以 JSON 提供，不需要 API key（[SEC：EDGAR API](https://www.sec.gov/search-filings/edgar-application-programming-interfaces)）。`companyconcept` 返回一家公司的一个标签（例如 `us-gaap/NetIncomeLoss`）；`companyfacts` 返回一家公司的全部标签；`frames` 对一个日历期间每家公司只返回一个值。公平访问：合计每秒不超过 **10 次请求**，脚本要表明身份；请求过多或身份不明的自动访问可能被 SEC 封禁（[SEC 开发者资源](https://www.sec.gov/about/developer-resources)）。

**每条数据都带着报出它的那份申报。** 每条记录有 `start`、`end`、`val`、`accn`（申报编号）、`fy`、`fp`、`form` 和 `filed`。同一个期间会在后来的申报里再次出现：下一年 10-K 里的对比数，以及修订版。一个真实例子（[Apple，NetIncomeLoss](https://data.sec.gov/api/xbrl/companyconcept/CIK0000320193/us-gaap/NetIncomeLoss.json)，2026-11-11 读取）：2008-09-28 至 2009-09-26 这个财年，2009-10-27 提交的 10-K 报 **57.04 亿美元**；2010-01-25 提交的 10-K/A 对同一年报 **82.35 亿美元**，2010 年和 2011 年的 10-K 都重复 82.35 亿。三个陷阱：

1. **按 `filed` 取。** 2009-11-02 的决策只能用 57.04 亿。取最新值就是前视。
2. **`frames` 返回最后一次提交的值。** SEC 说明 frames 接口对每家公司、每个期间取“最后提交”（last filed）的那条，所以它的 CY2009 值是 82.35 亿。看今天的快照没问题，做历史面板就错了。
3. **`fy` 和 `fp` 描述的是申报，不是期间。** 同一个 2009 财年的数字会带着 `fy=2010`、`fy=2011` 出现。期间要用 `start`/`end` 来认。

**回测规则：** 所有记录都保留；对每个决策日，取同一期间里 `filed` 早于该日的最新一条（存下 `accn` 以便追溯）；最早在 EDGAR 接收之后的下一个交易时段才能用（见上文 EDGAR 时间规则；`filed` 只有日期，接收时刻要从申报索引里取）。

**实验：** `examples/p2/17_xbrl_pit_10b51_toy.py`（四条记录抄自 SEC 的 JSON，不联网；B–C 节属于下面的内部人一节）：

```text
=== A. one period, four filings: pick by 'filed', not by 'fy' or 'frame' ===
  10-K   filed 2009-10-27 fy=2009 fp=FY val=5,704m frame=-
  10-K/A filed 2010-01-25 fy=2009 fp=FY val=8,235m frame=-
  10-K   filed 2010-10-27 fy=2010 fp=FY val=8,235m frame=-
  10-K   filed 2011-10-26 fy=2011 fp=FY val=8,235m frame=CY2009
decision 2009-10-20: not yet filed
decision 2009-11-02: 5704m from 10-K filed 2009-10-27
decision 2010-02-01: 8235m from 10-K/A filed 2010-01-25
frames API value for CY2009 = 8,235m (the last filed); using it on 2009-11-02 = look-ahead of the amendment
'fy' describes the FILING (the 2011 10-K repeats FY2009 as a comparative); identify periods by start/end.
=== B. Rule 10b5-1 cooling-off (directors/officers: later of 90 days or 2 business days after the 10-Q/10-K, max 120) ===
  officer, adopted 2026-02-10, Q1 10-Q filed 2026-05-06: cooling-off ends 2026-05-11
  officer, adopted 2026-03-25, Q1 10-Q filed 2026-05-06: cooling-off ends 2026-06-23
  officer, adopted 2026-01-05, Q1 10-Q filed 2026-05-08: cooling-off ends 2026-05-05
  other insider (not D&O), adopted 2026-02-10: cooling-off ends 2026-03-12
=== C. the Form 4 plan check box exists only for filings from 2023-04-01 ===
  Form 4 filed 2022-11-15: unknown (field did not exist; not the same as 'not a plan')
  Form 4 filed 2023-05-02: plan trade
  Form 4 filed 2024-08-20: not marked as a plan trade
assertions passed: 10/10
disclaimer: real public numbers in A (Apple FY2009 NetIncomeLoss) are for PIT mechanics only; no insider-trading signal; learning only
```

## 内部人和大股东的申报，什么时候才算“已知”（核实于 2026-11-10）

公司的董事、高管和持股超过 10% 的股东（统称“内部人”）买卖本公司股票，要报 Form 4（[investor.gov](https://www.investor.gov/introduction-investing/investing-basics/glossary/forms-3-4-and-5)），期限是执行日后第二个营业日结束前；某些计划交易按 16a-3(g)(4) 的视同执行日起算（[17 CFR 240.16a-3](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.16a-3)）。例如周五成交，最晚下周二报。持股超过 5% 的投资者一般要在五个营业日内报 13D，之后有重大变化要在两个营业日内修订；符合条件的机构投资者可以改报 13G，例如在季末后 45 天内报，13G 的期限因申报人类型而不同（[17 CFR 240.13d-1](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13d-1)，[SEC 2023-219](https://www.sec.gov/newsroom/press-releases/2023-219)）。

回测时，信号能用的时间是申报被 EDGAR 接收以后，不是表格里的交易日期，否则就提前偷看了通常最多两个营业日；视同执行日（16a-3(g)(4)）或迟报时更长。21:40（美东）被接收的申报算当天按时提交，但当天的常规交易 16:00 已经结束，所以日线回测最早只能在下一个交易时段使用。

还有一个 2026 年的变化：从 2026-03-18 起，在 Section 12 注册了股权证券的外国私人发行人的董事和高管也要报 Form 4，但 SEC 已对加拿大、欧洲经济区、英国、澳大利亚等若干法域发了豁免令（[SEC](https://www.sec.gov/about/divisions-offices/division-corporation-finance/holding-foreign-insiders-accountable-act-section-16a-reporting-requirements)）；名单可能变化，请以 SEC 页面为准，不要照抄。所以对这类公司，2026-03-18 之前“没有 Form 4”不代表内部人没有交易，只是当时不用报；之后也要先查它是否在豁免名单里。

**Rule 10b5-1 计划：2023-04-01 起 Form 4 上有一个勾选框。** 内部人常按事先制定的交易计划卖股，这类计划依据 Rule 10b5-1 设立，满足条件时可以作为内幕交易责任的抗辩。2022 年修订后，董事和高管按新计划交易之前要等**冷静期**结束：取“采用后 **90 天**”和“采用当季的 10-Q/10-K 披露后 **两个营业日**”中较晚的一个，但最长不超过 **120 天**；其他人（公司本身除外）是 **30 天**（[17 CFR 240.10b5-1(c)(1)(ii)(B)](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.10b5-1)）。Form 4 有一个勾选框，标明这笔交易是按意在满足 Rule 10b5-1(c) 的计划做的，并在说明栏写计划采用日（[Form 4 说明第 10 条](https://www.sec.gov/files/form4.pdf)）；2023-04-01 及以后提交的 Form 4 和 Form 5 必须填（[SEC 新闻稿 2022-222](https://www.sec.gov/newsroom/press-releases/2022-222)）。做数据时：(1) 这个框能把计划交易和其他交易分开；(2) 2023-04-01 之前没有这个字段，所以旧申报“没勾”表示**未知**，不表示“非计划交易”；(3) 计划内的卖出，至少在冷静期之前就定下来了。这里讲的是怎么正确标注数据；本课不给任何内部人交易信号。实验：`17_xbrl_pit_10b51_toy.py` 的 B–C 节（输出见上面的 XBRL 一节）。

**Form 4 的交易代码并不都是公开市场买卖；Form 3 是初次申报，Form 5 是年度补报（核实于 2026-11-12）。** Form 4 的*截止日期*（执行日后第二个营业日结束前，含视同执行日分支）已在上面讲过。要教官方代码表，回测才不会把每一笔 Form 4 取得都当成公开市场买入。[Form 4 一般说明第 8 条](https://www.sec.gov/files/form4.pdf)：“Use the code that most appropriately describes the transaction.”（使用最能恰当描述该笔交易的代码。）

- **一般代码：** **P** — “Open market or private purchase of non-derivative or derivative security”（公开市场或私下买入非衍生或衍生证券）；**S** — “Open market or private sale of non-derivative or derivative security”（公开市场或私下卖出）。在相应栏填 **V**，表示该笔交易“voluntarily reported earlier than required”（自愿早于期限申报）；否则该栏留空。不要把这个可选的提前申报标记当成买卖代码。
- **Rule 16b-3：** **A** — “Grant, award or other acquisition pursuant to Rule 16b-3(d)”（依据 Rule 16b-3(d) 的授予、奖励或其他取得）；**D** — “Disposition to the issuer of issuer equity securities pursuant to Rule 16b-3(e)”（依据 16b-3(e) 向发行人处分发行人股权证券）；**F** — “Payment of exercise price or tax liability by delivering or withholding securities incident to the receipt, exercise or vesting of a security issued in accordance with Rule 16b-3”（为支付行权价或税款而交付或扣留证券）；**I** — 依据 16b-3(f) 的酌情交易；**M** — “Exercise or conversion of derivative security exempted pursuant to Rule 16b-3”（依据 Rule 16b-3 获豁免的衍生证券行权或转换）。
- **其他已列出的代码还包括** C / E / H / O / X（衍生证券，16b-3 豁免的行权/转换除外）、**G** — “Bona fide gift”（真诚赠与）、L、W、Z、**J** — “Other acquisition or disposition (describe transaction)”（其他取得或处分，须说明）、**K** *附加*用于股权互换或类似工具（例如 “S/K” 或 “P/K”），以及 **U** — 控制权变更交易中的要约交存。

**Form 3 / Form 5 时钟。** [15 U.S.C. 78p(a)(2)(B)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/uscode/text/15/78p) 要求初次报表“within 10 days after he or she becomes such beneficial owner, director, or officer, or within such shorter time as the Commission may establish by rule”（在其成为该等实益所有人、董事或高管之后 10 天内，或在委员会以规则规定的更短期限内）。本课**不**发明更短的委员会期限；在 [17 CFR 240.16a-3](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.16a-3) 里没有找到这样的更短时钟。16a-3(a) 把初次报表指定为 Form 3，变动报表为 Form 4，年度报表为 Form 5。[16a-3(f)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.16a-3)：Form 5 “shall be filed within 45 days after the issuer's fiscal year end”（须在发行人财年结束后 45 天内提交），并报告此前未报的指定持仓和交易；“no Form 5 shall be required where all transactions otherwise required to be reported on the Form 5 have been reported before the due date”（若本应在 Form 5 上报告的交易均已在到期日前报告，则无须提交 Form 5）。这里的 “10 days” 和 “45 days” 按原文，本课不改写成营业日。若该日历截止日落在周六、周日或假日，Rule 0-3（申报时滞一节已经讲过）允许在随后第一个营业日提交——与 C 节里 13G 的 45 天时钟同一套 Rule 0-3 顺延。实验里，FYE 2025-12-31 加 45 个日历日是 2026-02-14（周六）；下一个周一 2026-02-16 是 [OPM 2026 年联邦假日日程](https://www.opm.gov/policy-data-oversight/pay-leave/federal-holidays/#url=2026) 上的华盛顿诞辰，因此可在 2026-02-17 提交。不要把 Form 13H 加进这个公开的 Section 16 / 13D / 13G 框（该表已作为保密申报讲过）。本课不给任何内部人交易信号。

**失去 13G 资格的 Schedule 13G 申报人须在五个营业日内改报 Schedule 13D；实益所有权是投票权和/或投资权，不只是登记在册的股份（核实于 2026-11-12）。** 上面已经讲过的 13D / 13G *初次申报时滞*在这里不改写。要教**转换**和 **13d-3** 的测试，回测才不会把一份仍未失效的 13G 当成“仍是被动 5% 持有人”的证明，也不会把登记在册的 4.9% 当成整个 13(d)/13(g) 头寸。

[17 CFR 240.13d-1(e)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.13d-1)：已按 (b) 或 (c) 款在 Schedule 13G 上报告超过 5%，或有义务报告该取得但尚未提交的人，“shall immediately become subject to paragraph (a) … and shall file a statement on Schedule 13D … **within five business days** if, and shall remain subject to those requirements for so long as, the person: (i) Has acquired or holds the securities with a purpose or effect of changing or influencing control of the issuer, … and (ii) Is at that time the beneficial owner of more than five percent ….”（若该人取得或持有证券的目的或效果是改变或影响发行人的控制权，并且当时仍是超过 5% 的实益所有人，则立即受 (a) 款约束，并须在**五个营业日**内提交 Schedule 13D）。从该目的或效果发生时起，“until the expiration of the **tenth day from the date of the filing** of the Schedule 13D,” 该人 “shall not: (i) Vote or direct the voting of the securities described therein; or (ii) Acquire an additional beneficial ownership interest in any equity securities of the issuer …, nor of any person controlling the issuer.”（直到该份 Schedule 13D **提交之日起第十日届满**之前，不得对其中所述证券表决或指示表决，也不得再取得该发行人或其控制人的额外股权实益所有权。）

[13d-1(f)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.13d-1)：按 **(c) 款**在 13G 上报告的人，须“**within five business days after the date on which the person's beneficial ownership equals or exceeds 20 percent** of the class” 提交 13D（在实益所有权达到或超过该类别 **20%** 之日后五个营业日内），并从达到 20% 时起适用同样的不得表决 / 不得再取得的冻结，直到 13D 提交之日起第十日。**不要**把这条 20% 上限套到每一名 13G 申报人身上；它只是 (c) 款路径。[13d-1(g)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.13d-1)：按 (b) 款申报的 13G 申报人若“ceases to be a person specified in paragraph (b)(1)(ii)”或“determines that it no longer has acquired or holds the securities in the ordinary course of business”，在仍超过 5% 时，须“**within five business days thereafter**” 视情形提交 13D 或 13G 修订。[13d-1(i)(2)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.13d-1)：“business day means any day, other than Saturday, Sunday, or a Federal holiday, from 12 a.m. to 11:59 p.m., Eastern Time.”（营业日指除周六、周日或联邦假日以外的任何一日，美东时间 0:00 至 23:59。）实验里，(b) 款申报人于 2026-10-05 丧失资格时，第五个营业日是 2026-10-13，因为 2026-10-12 是同一份 OPM 2026 年日程上的哥伦布日。

[17 CFR 240.13d-3(a)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.13d-3)：实益所有人“includes any person who, directly or indirectly, through any contract, arrangement, understanding, relationship, or otherwise has or shares: (1) Voting power … and/or, (2) Investment power ….”（包括任何直接或间接通过合同、安排、谅解、关系或其他方式拥有或共享投票权和/或投资权的人。）[13d-3(c)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.13d-3)：同一类别下实益拥有的全部证券“shall be aggregated”（应合并计算）。[13d-3(d)(1)(i)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.13d-3)：若某人有权在**六十日**内取得该证券的实益所有权（包括通过期权、权证、权利或转换），则视为实益所有人；但若该权利是“with the purpose or effect of changing or influencing the control of the issuer”而取得的，则在取得该权利时**立即**视为标的的实益所有人。此类尚未发行、但受期权、权证、权利或转换权约束的证券，在计算该人持股比例时视为已发行，在计算任何其他人的比例时不视为已发行。

不要复活 2023 年以前“10 个日历日”的*申报*时钟。现行转换申报时钟是五个营业日；“第十日”是 13D *提交之后*的冻结，从触发目的或达到 20% 时起，直到该次提交之日起第十日届满。不要把登记在册的股份当成整个 13(d)/13(g) 头寸。本课只教官方测试以及 60 日 / 立即计入控制目的这两支，不搭建完整的实益所有权合规引擎。不做维权或产品建议。

**实验：** `examples/p2/11_spinoff_insider_lag_toy.py` 的 C–D 节（玩具日历按 Rule 0-3 / 13d-1(i)(2) 把周六、周日和 OPM 2026 年官方联邦假日滚到随后第一个营业日；A–B 节见下面的分拆一节）：

```text

Form 4 trade 2026-11-06 (Fri) -> due by end of 2026-11-10 (Tue)

ASSERT_spinoff_insider_lag_ok
```

**Rule 13d-2：取得或处分 1% 即视为 13D 修订中的重大变化，应在两个营业日内修订；13G 一般在日历季度结束后 45 天内，若有重大变化才修订，并不走那条 13D 时钟（核实于 2026-11-12）。** 已经教过的“13D 在重大变化后两个营业日内修订”那一句、最初的 13D / 13G 时滞、转换时钟，以及 Form 10-S 提案句，保持原样。这一节补上官方的 1% 测试和官方的 13G 修订时钟，避免把过期的 13G 当成当季持仓，把不到 1% 的 13D 修订自动当成不重大，或把 13D 的 1% / 两个营业日时钟套到每一份 13G 上。没有申报人或披露建议。

[17 CFR 240.13d-2(a)](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13d-2)：如果 Schedule 13D 中陈述的事实发生任何重大变化，该人“应在该变化发生日后两个营业日内，向委员会提交或促使提交披露该变化的修订。”“取得或处分相当于该类证券百分之一或以上的实益所有权，就本条而言应视为‘重大’；少于该等数量的取得或处分，依事实和情形，仍可能是重大的。” 13d-2(b)：已按 13d-1(b)、(c) 或 (d) 提交 Schedule 13G 的人，“如在日历季度结束时，该 Schedule 上次申报的信息有任何重大变化，应在每个日历季度结束后 45 天内修订该声明”，前提是 (b) 或 (c) 款申报人仍满足那些要求；“如果先前报告的该类已发行百分比的变化，仅仅是因为已发行证券总数发生了变化，则不必就该变化提交修订。”一旦已提交反映实益所有权为该类 5% 或以下的修订，除非该人随后再次成为超过 5% 的实益所有人并须按 13d-1 申报，否则不必再提交。13d-2(c)（依赖 13d-1(b) 的人）：在 (b) 之外，在实益所有权（按该月最后一日计算）超过 10% 的第一个月份结束后五个营业日内，以及此后在月末增减超过 5% 后五个营业日内，提交 13G 修订。13d-2(d)（依赖 13d-1(c) 的人）：在 (b) 之外，在直接或间接取得超过 10% 后两个营业日内，以及此后在增减超过 5% 后两个营业日内，提交 13G 修订。

不要把 13D 的 1% / 两个营业日时钟套到每一份 13G 上。不要把仅仅因为已发行股数变化而导致的百分比变化，当成必须做的 13G 百分比修订。不要把每一次不到 1% 的 13D 变化都当成不重大。不要搭建完整的修订资格引擎；点名官方 (c)、(d) 分支并指向规则即可。不要重开 13d-1(e)/(f)/(g)、13d-3，或 Form 5 的 2026-02-17 / 13d-1(g) 的 2026-10-13 日期行。

**实验：** `examples/p2/11_spinoff_insider_lag_toy.py` 的 I 节（A–H 节的打印与此前相同，包括 Form 5 到期日 **2026-02-17** 和 13d-1(g) 的第五个营业日 **2026-10-13**，以及 H 节后的 54/54；全脚本 59 项断言）：

```text
=== I. Rule 13d-2: 1% is deemed material on a 13D; a 13G is not on that clock ===
13d-2(a): a Schedule 13D amendment disclosing a material change is due within two business days after the date of such change
an acquisition or disposition of beneficial ownership of one percent or more of the class is deemed material
acquisitions or dispositions of less than one percent may be material, depending upon the facts and circumstances
  1% acquisition or disposition deemed material? True
  a smaller change may still be material? True
a 13G is not on that same 1% / two-business-day clock
13d-2(b): a 13G filer under 13d-1(b), (c), or (d) generally amends within 45 days after calendar quarter-end
  if, as of the end of the calendar quarter, there are any material changes
  every 13G amends on the 13D 1% / two-business-day clock? False
a percent change solely from a change in the aggregate number of securities outstanding need not be a 13G amendment
  13G amendment required when the percent change is solely from shares outstanding? False
official 13d-2(c) and 13d-2(d) intra-period clocks also exist:
  13d-2(c) (13d-1(b) / QII filers): within five business days after month-end when beneficial ownership,
    computed as of the last day of the month, exceeds 10%, and thereafter after a more-than-5% increase or decrease
  13d-2(d) (13d-1(c) / passive filers): within two business days after acquiring greater than 10%,
    and thereafter within two business days after a more-than-5% increase or decrease
  this toy is not that engine
  this toy builds the 13d-2(c)/(d) engine? False
Form 5 due date 2026-02-17 and 13d-1(g) fifth business day 2026-10-13 remain as already printed
assertions passed: 59/59
ASSERT_spinoff_insider_lag_ok
disclaimer: official 13d-2 amendment clocks; no filer or disclosure advice; learning only
```

## 17 CFR 240.13d-4：实益所有权免责声明（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 13d-1 / 13d-2 / 13d-3 和已经打包的 12b-22 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：任何人都可以在所提交的任何报表中明确声明，该报表的提交不得被解释为承认该人是，为了 sections 13(d) or 13(g) of the Act 的目的，该报表所覆盖的任何证券的实益所有人。官方全文 “Any person may expressly declare in any statement filed that the filing of such statement shall not be construed as an admission that such person is, for the purposes of sections 13(d) or 13(g) of the Act, the beneficial owner of any securities covered by the statement.” 官方印刷是 “sections 13(d) or 13(g) of the Act”（是 “or，” 不是 “and”）。13d-4 免责声明并不是不存在实益所有权的认定，读 Schedule 13D / 13G 持有人数据时不能把它当成“查无所有权”。官方标题印刷是 “Disclaimer of beneficial ownership.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA；不要发明来源行。官方 SECAUTH 印刷是 “15 U.S.C. 78c(b), 78m(d)(1), 89m(d)(2), 78m(d)(5), 78m(d)(6), 78n(d)(1), 78w”，必须保留官方的 `89m(d)(2)`，不要改整齐成 `78m(d)(2)`。不要把本框压进已经打包的 13d-1 / 13d-2 / 13d-3，或已经打包的 12b-22（12b-22 只作为控制权免责声明的对照点名）。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.13d-4](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13d-4)：“Any person may expressly declare in any statement filed that the filing of such statement shall not be construed as an admission that such person is, for the purposes of sections 13(d) or 13(g) of the Act, the beneficial owner of any securities covered by the statement.” 官方标题印刷是 “Disclaimer of beneficial ownership.” 官方印刷是 “sections 13(d) or 13(g) of the Act。” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA。官方 SECAUTH 印刷包含 `89m(d)(2)`。Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 13d-1 / 13d-2 / 13d-3 和已经打包的 12b-22 仅点名。12b-22 只是控制权免责声明的对照。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 AX 节（接在第 AW 节 / 16a-7 之后；AX 之后为 179/179）：

```text
a 13d-4 disclaimer is not a finding of no beneficial ownership
official API XML has no CITA element; this toy does not invent a source line
official SECAUTH typography is 15 U.S.C. 78c(b), 78m(d)(1), 89m(d)(2), 78m(d)(5), 78m(d)(6), 78n(d)(1), 78w
official SECAUTH typography includes 89m(d)(2) and must not be tidied into 78m(d)(2)
cite 17 CFR 240.13d-4; title 17 up_to_date_as_of 2026-11-17
  an express disclaimer in a filed statement shall not be construed as an admission that such person is, for the purposes of sections 13(d) or 13(g) of the Act, the beneficial owner of any securities covered by the statement, and a 13d-4 disclaimer is not a finding of no beneficial ownership? True
```

## 17 CFR 240.13d-5：实益所有权的取得（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 13d-1 / 13d-2 / 13d-3 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则。官方 (a) 规定，成为证券实益所有人的人，为了 section 13(d)(1) 的目的，应被视为已取得该实益所有权，无论该取得是通过购买还是以其他方式；但死者遗产的执行人或管理人，一般被推定为在依当地法律取得任职资格之前，尚未取得该遗产持有的实益所有权。官方 (b)(1)(i) 是集团形成规则：两人或两人以上为取得、持有、投票或处分某发行人的股权证券而约定共同行动时，由此形成的集团应被视为已取得该发行人由其中任何人实益拥有的全部股权证券的实益所有权，为了 sections 13(d) and (g) of the Act 的目的，自该约定之日起算。官方 (b)(1)(ii)–(iii) 和官方 (b)(2)(i)–(ii) 再区分后来成员的增持与集团内部的出售或转让，并区分 section 13(d)(3) 集团与 section 13(g)(3) 集团。官方印刷是 “whether such acquisition was through purchase or otherwise，” “sections 13(d) and (g) of the Act，” “decedent's estate”（ASCII 撇号），以及 “through the earlier of {'{x}'} the date of the group's dissolution or {'{y}'} the date of that member's withdrawal from the group。” 官方 {'{x}'} / {'{y}'} 是 ASCII 花括号（U+007B/U+007D），不要改整齐成 `(x)` / `(y)`。官方来源行是 “[88 FR 76983, Nov. 7, 2023]”。官方标题印刷是 “Acquisition of beneficial ownership.” 不要把本框压进已经打包的 13d-3（实益所有人检验）或已经打包的 13d-1 / 13d-2。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.13d-5](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13d-5)：官方 (a) “A person who becomes a beneficial owner of securities shall be deemed to have acquired such beneficial ownership for purposes of section 13(d)(1) of the Act, whether such acquisition was through purchase or otherwise. However, executors or administrators of a decedent's estate generally will be presumed not to have acquired the beneficial ownership held by the decedent's estate until such time as such executors or administrators are qualified under local law to perform their duties.” 官方 (b)(1)(i) “When two or more persons agree to act together for the purpose of acquiring, holding, voting or disposing of equity securities of an issuer, the group formed thereby shall be deemed to have acquired beneficial ownership, for purposes of sections 13(d) and (g) of the Act, as of the date of such agreement, of all equity securities of that issuer beneficially owned by any such persons.” 官方 (b)(1)(ii) “A group regulated as a person pursuant to section 13(d)(3) of the Act shall be deemed to have acquired beneficial ownership, as determined under paragraph (a) of this section and for purposes of sections 13(d)(1) and (2) of the Act, if any member of the group becomes the beneficial owner of additional equity securities in the same class beneficially owned by the group after the group's formation. The beneficial ownership so acquired shall be reported as being held by the group through the earlier of {'{x}'} the date of the group's dissolution or {'{y}'} the date of that member's withdrawal from the group.” 官方 (b)(1)(iii) “Notwithstanding paragraph (b)(1)(ii) of this section, a group regulated under section 13(d)(3) of the Act shall not be deemed to have acquired beneficial ownership, as determined under paragraph (a) of this section, if, after the group's formation, a member of the group becomes the beneficial owner of additional equity securities in the same class beneficially owned by the group through a sale by or transfer from another member of the group.” 官方 (b)(2)(i) “A group regulated as a person pursuant to section 13(g)(3) of the Act shall be deemed to have become the beneficial owner, for purposes of sections 13(g)(1) and (2) of the Act, if any member of the group becomes a beneficial owner of additional equity securities in the same class held by the group after the group's formation and through the earlier of {'{x}'} the date of the group's dissolution or {'{y}'} the date of that member's withdrawal from the group.” 官方 (b)(2)(ii) “Notwithstanding paragraph (b)(2)(i) of this section, a group regulated under section 13(g)(3) of the Act shall not be deemed to have become the beneficial owner of additional equity securities in the same class beneficially owned by the group if, after the group's formation, a member of the group becomes the beneficial owner of additional equity securities in the same class beneficially owned by the group through a sale by or transfer from another member of the group.” 官方标题印刷是 “Acquisition of beneficial ownership.” 官方来源行是 “[88 FR 76983, Nov. 7, 2023]”。Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 13d-3 和已经打包的 13d-1 / 13d-2 仅点名。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 AY 节（接在第 AX 节 / 13d-4 之后；AY 之后为 184/184）：

## 17 CFR 240.13d-6：某些取得的豁免（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 13d-5 和已经打包的 13d-1 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则。官方 (a) 只在该人在此次取得之前已经是同一类别流通在外证券百分之五以上的实益所有人，并且该取得是依据向该类别全体持有人作出的发行中的优先认购权时，才豁免 section 13(d)；该人不得取得额外证券，除非是通过行使该人在这些权利中的按比例份额；并且该取得必须在需要时，依 section 16(a) 妥为申报。官方 (b) 是另一项单独的集团豁免，适用于与直接向发行人、且不涉及公开发行的股权证券购买有关的协同行动，但必须同时满足官方 (b)(1)–(4) 的全部条件。官方 (b)(1) 印刷是 “persons specified in § 240.13d-1(b)(1)(ii)。” 官方 13d-1(b)(1)(ii) 和官方 13d-3(b) 仅点名，不要打包那份目录。官方印刷是 “more than five percent，” “preemptive subscription rights，” “such person's pro rata share，” 以及 “duly reported, if required, pursuant to section 16(a) of the Act。” 官方标题印刷是 “Exemption of certain acquisitions.” 官方来源行是 “[88 FR 76984, Nov. 7, 2023]”，不要把页码改整齐成 13d-5 的 76983。不要把本框压进已经打包的 13d-5 或已经打包的 13d-1。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.13d-6](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13d-6)：官方 (a) “The acquisition of securities of an issuer by a person who, prior to such acquisition, was a beneficial owner of more than five percent of the outstanding securities of the same class as those acquired shall be exempt from section 13(d) of the Act; provided, that:” 官方 (a)(1) “The acquisition is made pursuant to preemptive subscription rights in an offering made to all holders of securities of the class to which the preemptive subscription rights pertain;” 官方 (a)(2) “Such person does not acquire additional securities except through the exercise of such person's pro rata share of the preemptive subscription rights; and” 官方 (a)(3) “The acquisition is duly reported, if required, pursuant to section 16(a) of the Act and the rules and regulations thereunder in this part.” 官方 (b) “A group shall be deemed not to have acquired any equity securities beneficially owned by the other members of the group solely by virtue of their concerted actions relating to the purchase of equity securities directly from an issuer in a transaction not involving a public offering; provided, that:” 官方 (b)(1) “All the members of the group are persons specified in § 240.13d-1(b)(1)(ii);” 官方 (b)(2) “The purchase is in the ordinary course of each member's business and not with the purpose nor with the effect of changing or influencing control of the issuer, nor in connection with or as a participant in any transaction having such purpose or effect, including any transaction subject to § 240.13d-3(b);” 官方 (b)(3) “There is no agreement among or between any members of the group to act together with respect to the issuer or its securities except for the purpose of facilitating the specific purchase involved; and” 官方 (b)(4) “The only actions among or between any members of the group with respect to the issuer or its securities subsequent to the closing date of the non-public offering are those which are necessary to conclude ministerial matters directly related to the completion of the offer or sale of the securities.” 官方标题印刷是 “Exemption of certain acquisitions.” 官方来源行是 “[88 FR 76984, Nov. 7, 2023]”。Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 13d-5 和已经打包的 13d-1 仅点名。官方 13d-1(b)(1)(ii) 和官方 13d-3(b) 仅点名。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 AZ 节（接在第 AY 节 / 13d-5 之后；AZ 之后为 189/189）：

```text
=== AZ. 17 CFR 240.13d-6: Exemption of certain acquisitions ===
official caption typography is Exemption of certain acquisitions.
13d-6(a): The acquisition of securities of an issuer by a person who, prior to such acquisition, was a beneficial owner of more than five percent of the outstanding securities of the same class as those acquired shall be exempt from section 13(d) of the Act; provided, that:
13d-6(a)(1): The acquisition is made pursuant to preemptive subscription rights in an offering made to all holders of securities of the class to which the preemptive subscription rights pertain;
13d-6(a)(2): Such person does not acquire additional securities except through the exercise of such person's pro rata share of the preemptive subscription rights; and
13d-6(a)(3): The acquisition is duly reported, if required, pursuant to section 16(a) of the Act and the rules and regulations thereunder in this part.
13d-6(b): A group shall be deemed not to have acquired any equity securities beneficially owned by the other members of the group solely by virtue of their concerted actions relating to the purchase of equity securities directly from an issuer in a transaction not involving a public offering; provided, that:
13d-6(b)(1): All the members of the group are persons specified in § 240.13d-1(b)(1)(ii);
13d-6(b)(2): The purchase is in the ordinary course of each member's business and not with the purpose nor with the effect of changing or influencing control of the issuer, nor in connection with or as a participant in any transaction having such purpose or effect, including any transaction subject to § 240.13d-3(b);
13d-6(b)(3): There is no agreement among or between any members of the group to act together with respect to the issuer or its securities except for the purpose of facilitating the specific purchase involved; and
13d-6(b)(4): The only actions among or between any members of the group with respect to the issuer or its securities subsequent to the closing date of the non-public offering are those which are necessary to conclude ministerial matters directly related to the completion of the offer or sale of the securities.
```

## 17 CFR 240.16a-6：小额取得（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的 Form 4 / Form 5 时钟、已经打包的 Section 16(b)，以及已经打包的 13d-2，保持原样。这一节是另一种申报递延：符合条件的小额取得（不是从发行人取得，也包括不是从发行人发起的员工福利计划取得），市值不超过 10,000 美元，并满足官方 (a)(1)–(2) 时，“shall be reported on Form 5.” 一旦不再符合条件，官方 (b) 要求在 “before the end of the second business day following the day on which the conditions of paragraph (a) of this section are no longer met” 之前用 Form 4 申报。不要把它改整齐成 “within two business days.” 不要把递延用到从发行人取得的证券上。不要把本规则压进那些已经上线的框。仅供学习；没有申报人、披露或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.16a-6](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.16a-6)：官方 (a) “Any acquisition of an equity security or the right to acquire such securities, other than an acquisition from the issuer (including an employee benefit plan sponsored by the issuer), not exceeding $10,000 in market value shall be reported on Form 5, subject to the following conditions:” 官方 (a)(1) 要把同一类别的其他取得（包括衍生工具标的证券，但不包括依规则豁免第 16(b) 条、或已在 Form 4 或 Form 5 上报过的取得）在此前六个月内合计，总额不得超过 10,000 美元市值。官方 (a)(2)：“The person making the acquisition does not within six months thereafter make any disposition, other than by a transaction exempt from section 16(b) of the Act.” 官方 (b)：“If an acquisition no longer qualifies for the reporting deferral in paragraph (a) of this section, all such acquisitions that have not yet been reported must be reported on Form 4 before the end of the second business day following the day on which the conditions of paragraph (a) of this section are no longer met.” 官方标题印刷是 “Small acquisitions.” 官方来源行：“[56 FR 7265, Feb. 21, 1991, as amended at 61 FR 30392, June 14, 1996; 67 FR 56467, Sept. 3, 2002].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。Form 5 仍为 2026-02-17，13d-1(g) 仍为 2026-10-13，与已经印出的日期相同。

已经教过的 Form 4 / Form 5 时钟、已经打包的 Section 16(b)，以及已经打包的 13d-2，仅点名，不另开核心。

**实验：** `examples/p2/11_spinoff_insider_lag_toy.py` 的第 J 节（接在第 I 节 / 13d-2 之后，并仍接在 tip 的 H 前缀之后；J 之后为 62/62）：

```text
=== J. 17 CFR 240.16a-6: Small acquisitions ===
official caption typography is Small acquisitions.
16a-6(a): Any acquisition of an equity security or the right to acquire such securities, other than an acquisition from the issuer (including an employee benefit plan sponsored by the issuer), not exceeding $10,000 in market value shall be reported on Form 5, subject to the following conditions:
16a-6(a)(1): Such acquisition, when aggregated with other acquisitions of securities of the same class (including securities underlying derivative securities, but excluding acquisitions exempted by rule from section 16(b) or previously reported on Form 4 or Form 5) within the prior six months, does not exceed a total of $10,000 in market value; and
16a-6(a)(2): The person making the acquisition does not within six months thereafter make any disposition, other than by a transaction exempt from section 16(b) of the Act.
16a-6(b): If an acquisition no longer qualifies for the reporting deferral in paragraph (a) of this section, all such acquisitions that have not yet been reported must be reported on Form 4 before the end of the second business day following the day on which the conditions of paragraph (a) of this section are no longer met.
```

## 17 CFR 240.16a-9：拆股、股票股利与按比例权利（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的拆股 / 分红价格调整、已经打包的 16a-6，以及已经打包的 Section 16(b)，保持原样。这一节是另一种豁免：因适用该类别全部证券的拆股或股票股利而发生的持有数量增加或减少，豁免第 16 条；向第 12 条注册的同一类别全部持有人按比例授予权利，其取得也豁免。官方 Note 是本打包切片的一部分：按比例权利的行权或出售 “shall be reported pursuant to § 240.16a-4.” 不要发明行权或出售也豁免申报。不要把本规则压进那些已经上线的框。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.16a-9](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.16a-9)：“The following shall be exempt from section 16 of the Act:” 官方 (a) “The increase or decrease in the number of securities held as a result of a stock split or stock dividend applying equally to all securities of a class, including a stock dividend in which equity securities of a different issuer are distributed; and” 官方 (b) “The acquisition of rights, such as shareholder or pre-emptive rights, pursuant to a pro rata grant to all holders of the same class of equity securities registered under section 12 of the Act.” 官方 Note：“The exercise or sale of a pro rata right shall be reported pursuant to § 240.16a-4 and the exercise shall be eligible for exemption from section 16(b) of the Act pursuant to § 240.16b-6(b).” 官方标题印刷是 “Stock splits, stock dividends, and pro rata rights.”（有牛津逗号）。官方来源行：“[56 FR 7265, Feb. 21, 1991, as amended at 61 FR 30393, June 14, 1996].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经教过的拆股 / 分红价格调整、已经打包的 16a-6、已经打包的 Section 16(b)，以及尚未打包的 16a-4 / 16b-6(b)，仅点名，不另开核心。

## 17 CFR 240.16a-11：股息或利息再投资计划（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的税务 DRIP / Pub 550 / Treas. Reg. 1.1012-1(e)、已经打包的 16a-6，以及已经打包的 16a-9，保持原样。这一节是另一种、第 16 条下的豁免：把**同一发行人**证券的股息或利息再投资而取得证券，如果计划规定定期再投资、面向广泛参与、不偏向发行人的员工，并且对所有参与者条款实质相同，则豁免第 16 条。不要发明每一个 DRIP 都豁免。不要把本规则压进税务 DRIP 框，或压进已经打包的 16a-6 / 16a-9。仅供学习；没有发行人、税务或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.16a-11](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.16a-11)：“Any acquisition of securities resulting from the reinvestment of dividends or interest on securities of the same issuer shall be exempt from section 16 of the Act if the acquisition is made pursuant to a plan providing for the regular reinvestment of dividends or interest and the plan provides for broad-based participation, does not discriminate in favor of employees of the issuer, and operates on substantially the same terms for all plan participants.” 官方标题印刷是 “Dividend or interest reinvestment plans.” 官方来源行：“[61 FR 30393, June 14, 1996].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

## 17 CFR 240.16a-4：衍生证券（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的 Form 4 交易代码、已经打包的 16a-6 / 16a-9 / 16a-11，以及已经打包的 Section 16(b)，保持原样。这一节打包整条现行规则，包括官方 Note。官方 (a) 印刷把 <em>except that</em> 做成斜体，斜体里没有末尾逗号（与已经打包的 15c1-3 / 15c1-9 不同）。官方 (b) 把买权等价头寸的行权或转换，按买入标的并了结衍生品来申报；官方 (c) 把卖权等价头寸按卖出标的并了结衍生品来申报。官方 (d) 指向尚未打包的 § 240.16b-6(d)。官方 Note 标题印刷是 Note to § 240.16<em>a</em>-4:，Note 只点名尚未打包的 § 240.16b-3 或 § 240.16b-6(b)。不要把这些尚未打包的规则当成核心来打包。不要把本规则压进已经教过的 Form 4 代码、已经打包的 16a-6 / 16a-9 / 16a-11，或已经打包的 Section 16(b)。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.16a-4](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.16a-4)：官方 (a) “For purposes of section 16 of the Act, both derivative securities and the underlying securities to which they relate shall be deemed to be the same class of equity securities, <em>except that</em> the acquisition or disposition of any derivative security shall be separately reported.” 官方 (b) “The exercise or conversion of a call equivalent position shall be reported on Form 4 and treated for reporting purposes as:” 官方 (b)(1) “A purchase of the underlying security; and” 官方 (b)(2) “A closing of the derivative security position.” 官方 (c) “The exercise or conversion of a put equivalent position shall be reported on Form 4 and treated for reporting purposes as:” 官方 (c)(1) “A sale of the underlying security; and” 官方 (c)(2) “A closing of the derivative security position.” 官方 (d) “The disposition or closing of a long derivative security position, as a result of cancellation or expiration, shall be exempt from section 16(a) of the Act if exempt from section 16(b) of the Act pursuant to § 240.16b-6(d).” 官方 Note 标题印刷是 Note to § 240.16<em>a</em>-4:。官方 Note：“A purchase or sale resulting from an exercise or conversion of a derivative security may be exempt from section 16(b) of the Act pursuant to § 240.16b-3 or § 240.16b-6(b)。” 官方标题印刷是 “Derivative securities.” 官方来源行：“[56 FR 7265, Feb. 21, 1991, as amended at 56 FR 19927, May 1, 1991; 61 FR 30392, June 14, 1996].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经教过的 Form 4 交易代码、已经打包的 16a-6 / 16a-9 / 16a-11、已经打包的 Section 16(b)，以及尚未打包的 16b-3 / 16b-6(b) / 16b-6(d)，仅点名。

## 17 CFR 240.16a-10：第 16(a) 条下的豁免（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 16a-6 / 16a-9 / 16a-11，以及已经打包的 Section 16(b)，保持原样。这一节是官方从 16(a) 通到 16(b) 的豁免衔接：“Except as provided in § 240.16a-6, any transaction exempted from the requirements of section 16(a) of the Act, insofar as it is otherwise subject to the provisions of section 16(b), shall be likewise exempt from section 16(b) of the Act。” 16a-6 是官方除外，不是 16a-10 下的 16(b) 豁免。不要把章节符号写丢，也不要把 “likewise exempt” 改写成 “also reported” 或 Form 5 递延。官方 API XML 没有来源行，不要发明一行。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 16a-6 / 16a-9 / 16a-11 / Section 16(b)。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

## 17 CFR 240.16a-13：实益所有权形式的变化（核实于 2026-11-19）

## 17 CFR 240.16a-12：家庭关系命令（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 16a-13 / 16a-10 / 16a-11、已经教过的 Form 4 交易代码，以及已经打包的 Section 16(b)，保持原样。这一节是另一种豁免：依据 domestic relations order 取得或处分股权证券，“as defined in the Internal Revenue Code or Title I of the Employee Retirement Income Security Act, or the rules thereunder，” “shall be exempt from section 16 of the Act。” 官方印刷是 “section 16 of the Act，” 不只是第 16(a) 条。不要把官方的 IRC / Title I ERISA 定义改整齐成光秃秃的 “ERISA QDRO。” 官方来源行是 “[61 FR 30393, June 14, 1996]。” 这与已经打包的 16a-13 是同一条官方来源行（同一日期、同一 FR 页码 30393）。不要发明不同的页码，也不要只因为共享同一条 CITA 就把两条规则合成一节。不要把本规则压进已经打包的 16a-13 / 16a-10 / 16a-11、已经教过的 Form 4 代码，或已经打包的 Section 16(b)，也不要发明这只是 Form 5 递延。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

## 17 CFR 240.16a-2：受第 16 条约束的人与交易（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 16a-12 / 16a-13 / 16a-10 / 16a-3 / 16a-11，以及已经打包的 16c-3 / 16c-4，保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：谁受 section 16 约束。官方开篇 “Any person who is the beneficial owner, directly or indirectly, of more than ten percent of any class of equity securities (“ten percent beneficial owner”) registered pursuant to section 12 of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>), any director or officer of the issuer of such securities, and any person specified in section 30(h) of the Investment Company Act of 1940 (15 U.S.C. 80a-29(h)), including any person specified in § 240.16a-8, shall be subject to the provisions of section 16 of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78p). The rules under section 16(a) of the Act do not apply to ten percent beneficial owners of an issuer that is a foreign private issuer, as defined in Rule 3b-4 (§ 240.3b-4 of this chapter). The rules under section 16 of the Act apply to any class of equity securities of an issuer whether or not registered under section 12 of the Act. The rules under section 16 of the Act also apply to non-equity securities as provided by the Investment Company Act of 1940. With respect to transactions by persons subject to section 16 of the Act:” 官方印刷是 “(“ten percent beneficial owner”)”，用弯引号。官方印刷是 “The rules under section 16(a) of the Act do not apply to ten percent beneficial owners of an issuer that is a foreign private issuer, as defined in Rule 3b-4 (§ 240.3b-4 of this chapter).” 官方印刷也是 “The rules under section 16 of the Act apply to any class of equity securities of an issuer whether or not registered under section 12 of the Act.” 不要把后一句改整齐进官方 (c)：官方 (c) 仍然规定，并非因其他原因受 section 16 约束的百分之十实益所有人，只需报告其作为已按 section 12 注册的发行人某类股权证券超过百分之十实益所有人期间所进行的交易。官方 (a)–(d) 以及 paragraph (b) 的 Note 一并打包。官方 16a-8 和官方 3b-4 仅点名。不要打包 16a-1，也不要把本框压进已经打包的 16a-3 / 16a-10 / 16a-12 或尚未打包的 16b-3 / 16b-6。官方来源行：“[56 FR 7265, Feb. 21, 1991, as amended at 61 FR 30392, June 14, 1996; 67 FR 43535, June 28, 2002; 76 FR 71877, Nov. 21, 2011; 91 FR 10333, Mar. 3, 2026; 91 FR 32336, June 1, 2026].” 仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.16a-2](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.16a-2)：官方开篇 “Any person who is the beneficial owner, directly or indirectly, of more than ten percent of any class of equity securities (“ten percent beneficial owner”) registered pursuant to section 12 of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>), any director or officer of the issuer of such securities, and any person specified in section 30(h) of the Investment Company Act of 1940 (15 U.S.C. 80a-29(h)), including any person specified in § 240.16a-8, shall be subject to the provisions of section 16 of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78p). The rules under section 16(a) of the Act do not apply to ten percent beneficial owners of an issuer that is a foreign private issuer, as defined in Rule 3b-4 (§ 240.3b-4 of this chapter). The rules under section 16 of the Act apply to any class of equity securities of an issuer whether or not registered under section 12 of the Act. The rules under section 16 of the Act also apply to non-equity securities as provided by the Investment Company Act of 1940. With respect to transactions by persons subject to section 16 of the Act:” 官方 (a) “A transaction(s) carried out by a director or officer in the six months prior to the director or officer becoming subject to section 16 of the Act shall be subject to section 16 of the Act and reported on the first required Form 4 only if the transaction(s) occurred within six months of the transaction giving rise to the Form 4 filing obligation and the director or officer became subject to section 16 of the Act solely as a result of the issuer registering a class of equity securities pursuant to section 12 of the Act.” 官方 (b) “A transaction(s) following the cessation of director or officer status shall be subject to section 16 of the Act only if:” 官方 (b)(1) “Executed within a period of less than six months of an opposite transaction subject to section 16(b) of the Act that occurred while that person was a director or officer; and” 官方 (b)(2) “Not otherwise exempted from section 16(b) of the Act pursuant to the provisions of this chapter.” 官方 Note to paragraph (<em>b</em>): “For purposes of this paragraph, an acquisition and a disposition each shall be an opposite transaction with respect to the other.” 官方 (c) “The transaction that results in a person becoming a ten percent beneficial owner is not subject to section 16 of the Act unless the person otherwise is subject to section 16 of the Act. A ten percent beneficial owner not otherwise subject to section 16 of the Act must report only those transactions conducted while the beneficial owner of more than ten percent of a class of equity securities of the issuer registered pursuant to section 12 of the Act.” 官方 (d)(1) “Transactions by a person or entity shall be exempt from the provisions of section 16 of the Act for the 12 months following appointment and qualification, to the extent such person or entity is acting as:” 官方 (d)(1)(i) “Executor or administrator of the estate of a decedent;” 官方 (d)(1)(ii) “Guardian or member of a committee for an incompetent;” 官方 (d)(1)(iii) “Receiver, trustee in bankruptcy, assignee for the benefit of creditors, conservator, liquidating agent, or other similar person duly authorized by law to administer the estate or assets of another person; or” 官方 (d)(1)(iv) “Fiduciary in a similar capacity.” 官方 (d)(2) “Transactions by such person or entity acting in a capacity specified in paragraph (d)(1) of this section after the period specified in that paragraph shall be subject to section 16 of the Act only where the estate, trust or other entity is a beneficial owner of more than ten percent of any class of equity security registered pursuant to section 12 of the Act.” 官方标题印刷是 “Persons and transactions subject to section 16.” 官方印刷是 “(“ten percent beneficial owner”)”，以及 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>，字母 l 保持官方斜体。官方来源行：“[56 FR 7265, Feb. 21, 1991, as amended at 61 FR 30392, June 14, 1996; 67 FR 43535, June 28, 2002; 76 FR 71877, Nov. 21, 2011; 91 FR 10333, Mar. 3, 2026; 91 FR 32336, June 1, 2026].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

官方 16a-8 和官方 3b-4 仅点名。已经打包的 16a-3 / 16a-10 / 16a-12 仅点名。

The rules under section 16 of the Act apply to any class of equity securities of an issuer whether or not registered under section 12 of the Act. The rules under section 16 of the Act also apply to non-equity securities as provided by the Investment Company Act of 1940. With respect to transactions by persons subject to section 16 of the Act:

## 17 CFR 240.3b-2：“officer” 的定义（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 16a-2 保持原样。这一节是《交易法》对 “officer” 的官方定义，不要把它和 3b-7 的 “executive officer” 合成一条，也不要把这里的印刷改整齐进仅点名的官方 16a-1(f)。官方全文 “The term <em>officer</em> means a president, vice president, secretary, treasury or principal financial officer, comptroller or principal accounting officer, and any person routinely performing corresponding functions with respect to any organization whether incorporated or unincorporated.” 官方印刷把 <em>officer</em> 写成斜体。官方印刷是 “treasury or principal financial officer”（不是 “treasurer”）和 “comptroller”（不是 “controller”）。该定义还包括任何经常履行相应职能的人，不论该组织是否注册成立。官方标题印刷是 `Definition of “officer”.`（“officer” 两侧是弯引号 U+201C/U+201D）。官方来源行是 “[47 FR 11464, Mar. 16, 1982; 47 FR 11819, Mar. 19, 1982]”。官方 16a-1 仅点名，不要打包 16a-1。不要把本框压进 3b-7。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.3b-2](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.3b-2)：“The term <em>officer</em> means a president, vice president, secretary, treasury or principal financial officer, comptroller or principal accounting officer, and any person routinely performing corresponding functions with respect to any organization whether incorporated or unincorporated.” 官方标题印刷是 `Definition of “officer”.` 官方印刷是 “treasury or principal financial officer” 和 “comptroller。” 官方来源行是 “[47 FR 11464, Mar. 16, 1982; 47 FR 11819, Mar. 19, 1982]”。Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

官方 16a-1 仅点名。不要把 3b-2 的印刷改整齐进官方 16a-1(f) 或 3b-7。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 BA 节（接在第 AZ 节 / 13d-6 之后；BA 之后为 193/193）：

```text
official caption typography is Definition of “officer”.
3b-2: The term officer means a president, vice president, secretary, treasury or principal financial officer, comptroller or principal accounting officer, and any person routinely performing corresponding functions with respect to any organization whether incorporated or unincorporated.

official typography is "treasury or principal financial officer" (not "treasurer")
official typography is "comptroller" (not "controller")

cite 17 CFR 240.3b-2; title 17 up_to_date_as_of 2026-11-17
```

## 17 CFR 240.3b-7：“executive officer” 的定义（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 3b-2 和已经打包的 16a-2 保持原样。“officer” 与 “executive officer” 是两条官方上不同的定义，不要合成一条，也不要把这里的印刷改整齐进仅点名的官方 16a-1(f)（那里用的是 “policy-making、” “vice-president” 和 “controller”）。官方全文 “The term <em>executive officer,</em> when used with reference to a registrant, means its president, any vice president of the registrant in charge of a principal business unit, division or function (such as sales, administration or finance), any other officer who performs a policy making function or any other person who performs similar policy making functions for the registrant. Executive officers of subsidiaries may be deemed executive officers of the registrant if they perform such policy making functions for the registrant.” 官方印刷把 <em>executive officer,</em> 写成斜体，并且斜体包含逗号。副总裁只有在主管一项主要业务单位、部门或职能（例如销售、行政或财务），或在其他方面履行政策制定职能时才算。官方印刷是 “policy making”（两个词，没有连字符）和 “vice president”（两个词，没有连字符）。子公司的 executive officers 如果为注册人履行这类 policy making 职能，也可被视为注册人的 executive officers。官方标题印刷是 `Definition of “executive officer”.`（弯引号 U+201C/U+201D）。官方来源行是 “[47 FR 11464, Mar. 16, 1982, as amended at 56 FR 7265, Feb. 21, 1991]”。官方 16a-1 仅点名，不要打包 16a-1。不要把本框压进 3b-2。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.3b-7](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.3b-7)：“The term <em>executive officer,</em> when used with reference to a registrant, means its president, any vice president of the registrant in charge of a principal business unit, division or function (such as sales, administration or finance), any other officer who performs a policy making function or any other person who performs similar policy making functions for the registrant. Executive officers of subsidiaries may be deemed executive officers of the registrant if they perform such policy making functions for the registrant.” 官方标题印刷是 `Definition of “executive officer”.` 官方印刷是 “policy making” 和 “vice president。” 官方来源行是 “[47 FR 11464, Mar. 16, 1982, as amended at 56 FR 7265, Feb. 21, 1991]”。Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

官方 16a-1 仅点名。不要把 3b-7 的印刷改整齐进官方 16a-1(f) 或 3b-2。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 BB 节（接在第 BA 节 / 3b-2 之后；BB 之后为 197/197）：

Executive officers of subsidiaries may be deemed executive officers of the registrant if they perform such policy making functions for the registrant.

## 17 CFR 240.16c-3：待取得证券出售的豁免（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经教过的当发行 / 当分配框和已经打包的 16a-4 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则：官方 (a) “Whenever any person is entitled, incident to ownership of an issued security and without the payment of consideration, to receive another security “when issued” or “when distributed,” the sale of the security to be acquired shall be exempt from the operation of section 16(c) of the Act: <em>Provided,</em> That:” 官方 (a)(1) “The sale is made subject to the same conditions as those attaching to the right of acquisition;” 官方 (a)(2) “Such person exercises reasonable diligence to deliver such security to the purchaser promptly after the right of acquisition matures; and” 官方 (a)(3) “Such person reports the sale on the appropriate form for reporting transactions by persons subject to section 16(a) of the Act.” 官方印刷把 “Provided,” 做成斜体，包括逗号，写作 “<em>Provided,</em> That:”。官方印刷在 “when issued” 和 “when distributed” 两侧使用弯引号。官方 (b) “This section shall not exempt transactions involving both a sale of the issued security and a sale of a security “when issued” or “when distributed” if the combined transactions result in a sale of more securities than the aggregate of issued securities owned by the seller plus those to be received for the other security “when issued” or “when distributed.”” 官方标题印刷是 “Exemption of sales of securities to be acquired.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA；不要发明来源行。不要把本框压进已经教过的当发行框或已经打包的 16a-4。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.16c-3](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.16c-3)：官方 (a) “Whenever any person is entitled, incident to ownership of an issued security and without the payment of consideration, to receive another security “when issued” or “when distributed,” the sale of the security to be acquired shall be exempt from the operation of section 16(c) of the Act: <em>Provided,</em> That:” 官方 (a)(1) “The sale is made subject to the same conditions as those attaching to the right of acquisition;” 官方 (a)(2) “Such person exercises reasonable diligence to deliver such security to the purchaser promptly after the right of acquisition matures; and” 官方 (a)(3) “Such person reports the sale on the appropriate form for reporting transactions by persons subject to section 16(a) of the Act.” 官方 (b) “This section shall not exempt transactions involving both a sale of the issued security and a sale of a security “when issued” or “when distributed” if the combined transactions result in a sale of more securities than the aggregate of issued securities owned by the seller plus those to be received for the other security “when issued” or “when distributed.”” 官方标题印刷是 “Exemption of sales of securities to be acquired.” 官方印刷把 “Provided,” 做成斜体，包括逗号。官方印刷在 “when issued” 和 “when distributed” 两侧使用弯引号。官方 API XML 没有 CITA。Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经教过的当发行框和已经打包的 16a-4 仅点名。

**实验：** 同一脚本的第 AP 节（接在第 AO 节 / 232.406 之后；AP 之后为 149/149）：

## 17 CFR 240.16c-4：衍生证券（核实于 2026-11-19）

## 17 CFR 240.16c-2：与分销相关的交易（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 16c-3 和已经打包的 16c-4 保持原样。这一节是另一条 section 16(c) 豁免。官方标题印刷是 “Transactions effected in connection with a distribution.”——与 16a-7 用词相同——但这是 section 16(c) 的豁免，不要改整齐进 16a-7。要打包官方**整段**，包括两个官方替代条件：超额配售条款，以及 “or the dealer or a person acting on the dealer's behalf intends in good faith to offset such sale with a security to be acquired by or on behalf of the dealer as a participant in an underwriting, selling, or soliciting-dealer group of which the dealer is a member at the time of the sale, whether or not the security to be acquired is subject to a prior offering to existing security holders or some other class of persons.” 官方印刷是 “the dealer's behalf”（ASCII 撇号）。不要丢掉第二个替代条件，也不要改写官方 “dealer's” 的标点。官方全文 “Any transaction shall be exempt from section 16(c) of the Act to the extent necessary to render lawful any sale made by or on behalf of a dealer in connection with a distribution of a substantial block of securities, where the sale is represented by an over-allotment in which the dealer is participating as a member of an underwriting group, or the dealer or a person acting on the dealer's behalf intends in good faith to offset such sale with a security to be acquired by or on behalf of the dealer as a participant in an underwriting, selling, or soliciting-dealer group of which the dealer is a member at the time of the sale, whether or not the security to be acquired is subject to a prior offering to existing security holders or some other class of persons.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA；不要发明来源行。不要把本框压进已经打包的 16c-3 或已经打包的 16c-4。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.16c-2](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.16c-2)：“Any transaction shall be exempt from section 16(c) of the Act to the extent necessary to render lawful any sale made by or on behalf of a dealer in connection with a distribution of a substantial block of securities, where the sale is represented by an over-allotment in which the dealer is participating as a member of an underwriting group, or the dealer or a person acting on the dealer's behalf intends in good faith to offset such sale with a security to be acquired by or on behalf of the dealer as a participant in an underwriting, selling, or soliciting-dealer group of which the dealer is a member at the time of the sale, whether or not the security to be acquired is subject to a prior offering to existing security holders or some other class of persons.” 官方标题印刷是 “Transactions effected in connection with a distribution.”——与 16a-7 用词相同——但这是 section 16(c) 的豁免。官方印刷是 “the dealer's behalf”（ASCII 撇号）。官方 API XML 没有 CITA。Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 16c-3 和已经打包的 16c-4 仅点名。不要把本框与 16a-7 合成一节。

## 17 CFR 240.16a-7：与分销相关的交易（核实于 2026-11-19）

已经打包的 16a-6 和已经打包的 16a-10 保持原样。这一节是另一条规则。官方标题印刷与 16c-2 用词相同，但这是 section 16(a) 的申报豁免，不要改整齐进 16c-2。官方 (a) “Any purchase and sale, or sale and purchase, of a security that is made in connection with the distribution of a substantial block of securities shall be exempt from the provisions of section 16(a) of the Act, to the extent specified in this rule, subject to the following conditions:” 官方 (a)(1) “The person effecting the transaction is engaged in the business of distributing securities and is participating in good faith, in the ordinary course of such business, in the distribution of such block of securities; and” 官方 (a)(2) “The security involved in the transaction is:” 官方 (a)(2)(i) “Part of such block of securities and is acquired by the person effecting the transaction, with a view to distribution thereof, from the issuer or other person on whose behalf such securities are being distributed or from a person who is participating in good faith in the distribution of such block of securities; or” 官方 (a)(2)(ii) “A security purchased in good faith by or for the account of the person effecting the transaction for the purpose of stabilizing the market price of securities of the class being distributed or to cover an over-allotment or other short position created in connection with such distribution.” 官方 (b) “Each person participating in the transaction must qualify on an individual basis for an exemption pursuant to this section.” 官方印刷是 “Any purchase and sale, or sale and purchase.” 官方 (b) 印刷是 “Each person participating in the transaction must qualify on an individual basis for an exemption pursuant to this section.” 官方标题印刷是 “Transactions effected in connection with a distribution.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA；不要发明来源行。不要把本框压进 16c-2，或已经打包的 16a-6 / 16a-10。仅供学习；没有发行人、申报人或投资建议。

[17 CFR 240.16a-7](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.16a-7)：官方 (a) “Any purchase and sale, or sale and purchase, of a security that is made in connection with the distribution of a substantial block of securities shall be exempt from the provisions of section 16(a) of the Act, to the extent specified in this rule, subject to the following conditions:” 官方 (a)(1) “The person effecting the transaction is engaged in the business of distributing securities and is participating in good faith, in the ordinary course of such business, in the distribution of such block of securities; and” 官方 (a)(2) “The security involved in the transaction is:” 官方 (a)(2)(i) “Part of such block of securities and is acquired by the person effecting the transaction, with a view to distribution thereof, from the issuer or other person on whose behalf such securities are being distributed or from a person who is participating in good faith in the distribution of such block of securities; or” 官方 (a)(2)(ii) “A security purchased in good faith by or for the account of the person effecting the transaction for the purpose of stabilizing the market price of securities of the class being distributed or to cover an over-allotment or other short position created in connection with such distribution.” 官方 (b) “Each person participating in the transaction must qualify on an individual basis for an exemption pursuant to this section.” 官方标题印刷是 “Transactions effected in connection with a distribution.”——与 16c-2 用词相同——但这是 section 16(a) 的豁免。官方印刷是 “Any purchase and sale, or sale and purchase.” 官方 API XML 没有 CITA。Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17。

已经打包的 16a-6 和已经打包的 16a-10 仅点名。不要把本框与 16c-2 合成一节。
